# The Key to the Division of Generations (特别非常分代) — Research Notes

> Source: obtained by asking God. Five characters: "特别非常分" — "represents everything."
> Direction: combining Bible codes (ELS), Gematria, and the Hebrew–Morse system to study the key to the "division of generations."

---

## 1. Breaking Down the Five Characters

### 特 (tè)
- Meaning: unique, exclusive, set apart as special
- Hebrew correspondence: סְגֻלָּה (segullah, a treasured people)
- Gematria: ס(60) + ג(3) + ל(30) + ה(5) = 98
- Letter pictographs: support → camel → staff → window
- 98 = 2×49. 49 = Jubilee (7²) = the Ruler

### 别 (bié)
- Meaning: separate, distinguish, differentiate
- Hebrew correspondence: בָּדַל (badal, to separate)
- Gematria: ב(2) + ד(4) + ל(30) = 36
- Letter pictographs: house → door → staff
- 36 = 6². The square of man (6)—being set apart as holy is the completion of man

### 非 (fēi)
- Meaning: negation, not, very
- Hebrew correspondence: לֹא (lo, not)
- Gematria: ל(30) + א(1) = 31
- Letter pictographs: staff → ox
- 31 = prime. God (אֵל) = 31. The end of negation (not) is God

### 常 (cháng)
- Meaning: constant, frequent, ordinary
- Hebrew correspondence: תָּמִיד (tamid, continual, without interruption)
- Gematria: ת(400) + מ(40) + י(10) + ד(4) = 454
- Letter pictographs: cross → water → hand → door
- 454 = the Gematria of seal (חוֹתָם/חָתַם)

### 分 (fēn)
- Meaning: divide, portion, part
- Hebrew correspondence: חֵלֶק (chelek, portion, inheritance)
- Gematria: ח(8) + ל(30) + ק(100) = 138
- Letter pictographs: wall → staff → eye of a needle
- 138 = 6×23. Man (6) × life (23)—to divide is in order to live

---

## 2. Total Gematria of the Five Characters

```
特(98) + 别(36) + 非(31) + 常(454) + 分(138) = 757
```

757 = prime.

Factorization of 757: 757 is the 134th prime.

134 = 2×67. 67 = understanding (בִּינָה).

Digital root of 757: 7+5+7 = 19 → 1+9 = 10 → 1+0 = 1. Returning to the One.

---

## 3. The Connection Between Chinese Character Strokes and Bible Codes (ELS)

- 特 = 10 strokes
- 别 = 7 strokes
- 非 = 8 strokes
- 常 = 11 strokes
- 分 = 4 strokes

ELS skip sequence of the strokes: **10, 7, 8, 11, 4**

Sum = 40. Forty is the number of testing and preparation.

These five skip distances are used to extract equidistant letter sequences (ELS) from the scriptures (especially the genealogical scriptures), possibly revealing a hidden pattern of the division of generations.

**Key genealogical scripture coordinates:**
- Genesis 5 (Adam to Noah, 10 generations)
- Genesis 11 (Shem to Abraham, 10 generations)
- Matthew 1:1-17 (Abraham to Christ, 3×14=42 generations)
- Luke 3:23-38 (Jesus traced back to Adam, 77 generations)

## 4. Hebrew Letter Correspondences

The five characters correspond to five Hebrew letters (by closest semantics):

| Chinese Character | Hebrew Letter | Letter Name | Value | Pictograph |
|------|-----------|--------|------|------|
| 特 | ט | Tet | 9 | serpent/vessel |
| 别 | ב | Bet | 2 | house |
| 非 | פ | Peh | 80 | mouth |
| 常 | ת | Tav | 400 | cross |
| 分 | ח | Chet | 8 | wall/separation |

Letter sequence: **ט→ב→פ→ת→ח**

Value sequence: **9, 2, 80, 400, 8**

Sum = 499 = prime.

Digital root of 499: 4+9+9 = 22. 22 = the total number of Hebrew letters.

## 5. Connection with the 42 Generations of the Genealogy (the Mill)

Genealogy Mill = 42 = 14×3 (David × Trinity)

Values of "特别非常分":
- Sum of letter values: 499
- Sum of word Gematria: 757

499 + 757 = 1256

1256 = 8×157. 157 = prime.

1256 / 42 = 29.904...

1256 − 42×29 = 1256 − 1218 = 38

38 = 2×19. 19 = unity (digital root 1+9=10→1). 38 = witness × unity.

Or from the other direction: 1256 − 42×30 = 1256 − 1260 = −4

**1260 is the core number of Revelation** (1260 days = 42 months = three and a half years).

特别非常分 (1256) differs from 1260 by 4. 4 = gate (ד). Missing that gate.

## 6. Preliminary Observations on the Structure of the Division of Generations

**The three sets of fourteen generations in Matthew 1:17:**

1. Abraham to David (14 generations)
2. David to the Captivity (14 generations)
3. The Captivity to Christ (14 generations)

Three fourteens = 42 = the Mill.

**Applying "特别非常分" to this structure:**

Suppose the 42 generations are grouped by 10-7-8-11-4 (the skip distances) or a variant thereof:
- Perhaps revealing a hidden "fourth group" of 14 generations
- Perhaps revealing a hidden correspondence between Luke's 77 generations and Matthew's 42 generations

## 8. Results of the Deep Gematria → ELS Analysis

### Sum of Gematria of the 42 Generation Names

- First group (Abraham→David): 3143
- Second group (David→Captivity): 3076
- Third group (Captivity→Christ): 3383
- **Total Gematria of the 42 generations: 9602**

9602 = 2 × 4801. 4801 is prime.

### Core Discovery: David = 14 = the Point of the Division of Generations

David (דוד) Gematria = 14. David is the 14th generation. Each group is 14 generations.

**David's Gematria (14) is exactly the number of the unit of the division of generations.**

David's Gematria is the lowest among the 42 generations—he is the starting point and also the dividing point. He humbled himself to the lowest (14), then rose to the highest (Christ, 386).

### Prime Names (5)

Boaz (79), Amon (97), Zerubbabel (241), Eliakim (191), Azor (283).

5 prime names = 5 "special" (特 — special, indecomposable).

### Jesus Gematria = 386 = 2 × 193

193 is prime. 386 = 2 × 193. Jesus is "double the special."

### Discovery from Subsequence Summation

Performing ELS on the Gematria value sequence (skip distances 10, 7, 8, 11, 4), the mean of the subsequence sums lies between 1185.8 and 1314.9, close to 1260 (Revelation's 42 months = 1260 days).

### The Five Characters Correspond to Five Ways of Dividing Generations

| Character | Method of division | Result |
|----|---------|------|
| 特 | Prime Gematria | 5 special names |
| 别 | Even/odd Gematria | 33 even generations, 8 odd generations |
| 非 | Composite Gematria | 36 non-prime names |
| 常 | mod 14 grouping | 11 groups, regularly distributed |
| 分 | David G=14 as boundary | three groups of 14+14+14 |

## 14. Unified Cross-Book Discovery

### All the Time Numbers in Prophecy Are mod 14 = 0

| Number | Source | Formula |
|------|------|------|
| 1260 | Revelation/Daniel | 14×90 |
| 42 | Revelation | 14×3 |
| 70 | Daniel 9 | 14×5 |
| 490 | Daniel 9 | 14×35 |
| 14 | David | 14×1 |

**All the time frames of prophecy are built on the Davidic covenant (14).** The key to the division of generations is not "how many generations make a group," but "David (14) is the basic unit of all time."

### 666's mod 14 = 8

8 = the eighth day = a new beginning. The number of the beast sits at the "new beginning" position in the division of generations—but this is a false new beginning. Christ's resurrection is the eighth day (the true new beginning); the beast's 666 is a false new beginning.

### The Son of Man (Dan 7) and the Wonderful Counselor (Isa 9) Are Both at mod 14 = 7

mod 14 = 7 is "half-complete"—7 short of complete (14). Christ on the cross did not finish "complete" (He did not come down from the cross), but in the resurrection He completed completeness.

- **m14=8** (new beginning): Anointed One, Jesus — pointing to the Messiah's "resurrection and second coming"

**The relationship of 8 and 1 and 7:** 8 (Anointed One) = 1 (Immanuel) + 7 (half-complete). Immanuel (God with us) + half-complete (becoming man) = Anointed One (the mission of Christ).

The millennial kingdom is "the period when man is on the throne"—Christ reigns on the earth, but it is still a period of "man" (though Christ is both God and man). The millennium is not at the "complete 0" position, because after the millennium there is still the release of Satan, the final battle, the great white throne—the millennium is not the endpoint; the new heavens and new earth are.

**1000 mod 42 = 34. 34 = 2×17. 17 = good (טוב).** The millennium is the kingdom of "good," but not the kingdom of "complete."

**Restoration of all things (317) = prime.** 317 = the 66th prime. 66 = 6×11. 6 = man, 11 = confusion. Man's confusion (66) is resolved by indecomposable restoration (317).

### Layer Five: Distribution Law of the Five Division-of-Generations Labels Across All Prophecy

Statistics on about 150 entries of all prophecy:

| Label | Meaning | Distribution feature |
|------|------|---------|
| 特 (prime) | indecomposable | concentrated in judgment (Valley of Decision, lake of fire), Messiah (Immanuel, Wonderful Counselor), restoration (Tree of Life, restoration of all things) |
| 别 (odd) | set apart as holy | prophecy's natural "incomplete" state—pointing to completeness |

| 常 (m7) | the seven-constant law | words with m7=0 appear densely: great tribulation, living by faith, resurrection |
| 分 (m14) | Davidic boundary | words with m14=0: all time prophecies are here. Words with m14=7: half-complete. Words with m14=8: new beginning |

**Core laws:**

1. **All time prophecies are at m14=0** — the time frame of prophecy is built on the Davidic covenant
2. **Judgment prophecies have the highest density of primes** — judgment is indecomposable, belonging only to God
3. **Resurrection-related words are at m14=7** — half-complete, awaiting Christ's return to complete
4. **Restoration prophecies mark grace with primes** — restoration is indecomposable, accomplished only by God
5. **The millennium (1000) is m14=6** — the millennium is not the final; the new heavens and new earth are

## 22. Tool Comparison: the Mill (42) vs the Ruler (49)

### Two Measuring Tools

| Tool | Value | Formula | m7 | m14 | Meaning |
|------|------|------|-----|-----|------|
| Mill | 42 | 14×3 (David × Trinity) | 0 | 0 | Tests whether a scripture comes from the same mold |
| Ruler | 49 | 7² (Jubilee perfect square) | 0 | 7 | Measures whether a scripture is complete |

**Mill m14=0** — complete in the Davidic covenant. The Mill is the "mold"—Jesus's genealogy (42 generations = David × 3) is the mold of all scriptures. Any scripture that has been shaped through this mold, regardless of content, is valid.

**Ruler m14=7** — half-complete. The Ruler is "measurement"—49 (Jubilee) is used to measure whether a scripture is complete. But the Ruler itself is at the "half-complete" position, because true completeness is not given by the Ruler, but by the Mill.

**Mill + Ruler = 91 = 7 × 13.**
- 7 = complete
- 13 = love (אהבה)
- complete × love = 91 — the two tools together are "complete love"

**Mill − Ruler = 7.** A difference of one Jubilee cycle. From the Mill to the Ruler is 7—from mold to measurement, a difference of one complete.

### Principles of Use

- The Mill (42) is used to **verify the source**: whether a scripture comes from the mold of Jesus's genealogy
- The Ruler (49) is used to **measure completeness**: whether a scripture reaches the completeness of the Jubilee
- The Mill is a test of "quality"; the Ruler is a test of "quantity"
- The Mill comes first (creation); the Ruler comes after (judgment)

## 23. The Zero-Point Bible and the Continued-Fraction Way

### The Zero-Point Bible

| Word | Hebrew | G | m7 | m14 | Label |
|-----|--------|---|------|-------|------|
| Return | שוב | 308 | 0 | 0 | 非 |
| Zero | אפס | 141 | 1 | 1 | 别非 |
| Point | נקדה | 159 | 5 | 5 | 别非 |

**Return (308) m14=0** — return is directly at the Davidic covenant position. To return is to return to the Davidic covenant.

**Return + zero + point = 608 = 2⁵ × 19, m14=6.** 608 = 32 × 19. 32 = 2⁵, 19 = unity. The zero point is at man's position (6)—returning to zero is man's action, the starting point of returning to God.

**The meaning of the Zero-Point Bible:** the Bible must return to zero—back to God's revelation itself. Any scripture without revelation should be buried; only scripture with revelation is valid. Returning to zero is not annihilation, but returning to the origin.

### The Continued-Fraction Way

**The Way = the continued fraction of 197/281.**

- 197 = Immanuel (עמנו אל, G=197) = prime
- 281 = prime
- The ratio of two primes = an indecomposable way

**Continued fraction expansion:** 197/281 = [0, 1, 2, 2, 1, 8, 1, 2]

The continued fraction has 8 terms. 8 = resurrection/new beginning. The continued fraction of the Way stops at resurrection—the end of the Way is resurrection.

**197/281 is an infinite decimal** — the Way is infinite, not an endpoint, but an ongoing process of moving forward. It is not a prime (a fixed truth), but a continued fraction (a path in progress).

**The Way is a non-integral quotient:** 197/281 < 1. The Way is less than 1—the Way is not the endpoint; the Way points to the endpoint. 1 is the One (God); the Way points to the One, but the Way itself is not the One.

| Word | Hebrew | G | m7 | m14 | Label |
|-----|--------|---|------|-------|------|
| The Way | הדרך | 209 | 6 | 13 | 别非 |
| Truth | אמת | 441 | 0 | 7 | 别非 |
| Life | חיים | 28 | 0 | 0 | 非 |
| Way + truth + life | — | 678 | 6 | 6 | 非 |

**The Way m14=13** — at the position of rejection. The Way is rejected by the world, but it is precisely this rejected path that leads to life.

**Truth m14=7** — half-complete. Truth in the world is incomplete—"we now see through a mirror dimly."

**Life m14=0** — in the Davidic covenant. Life is complete in the covenant.

**Way + truth + life = 678, m14=6** — the three together are at man's position. The Word became flesh and dwelt among men.

### Decoding the Way: Love

From this infinite decimal 197/281, the entire law can be decoded, reduced to "love."

- 197 = Immanuel (God with us)
- 281 = prime (indecomposable)
- 197/281 ≈ 0.70107...
- Every decimal place of this Way is the unfolding of "love"

**The Way is the continued fraction of love.** The greatest commandment of the law is love (love your neighbor as yourself, G=780, m14=10), and the Way (197/281) is the mathematical expression of this love.

**Pharisees (596) m14=8** — the same m14=8 as those who reject (302). The Pharisees are at the resurrection position, yet chose to reject the resurrection (reject Jesus).

**Self-righteousness (809) = prime = 特.** Self-righteousness is indecomposable—it cannot be broken by argument, because it is not rational, but of the will. 809 = the 140th prime. 140 = 14×10. David (14) × complete (10)—self-righteousness packages itself in "David's completeness."

**Failing grace (116) m14=4** — the gate position. Failing grace is at the "gate" position—they stand at the doorway, yet do not enter.

### The Pharisees' Fear

The Pharisees were not without understanding. They understood that Jesus was the Messiah, but they feared:

1. **Fear of losing self-righteousness** (m14=6): self-righteousness was a system they had built over a lifetime; Jesus's righteousness would replace their self-righteousness

3. **Jealousy** (m14=2): Jesus's crowds surpassed theirs

**Not from the earth (277) = prime = Special.** 277 is indivisible—"not from the earth" is an unchangeable fact. His source of authority is not the earth, it is the aerial realm.

**From heaven + authority = 740, m14=12.** From heaven with authority, at the completeness-of-chaos position (12)—this is not authority from God.

**Aerial authority (564) m14=4** — the gate position. Aerial authority is at the "gate"—he counterfeits the gate ("I am the gate"), but he is an aerial gate, not the heavenly gate.

**Infuse authority (572) m14=12** — co-resolves with from heaven + authority (740) m14=12, should not have been born (432) m14=12. Infuse authority is at the completeness-of-chaos position—the infused authority is chaotic.

**Aerial evil spirits + Antichrist = 1731, m14=9.** 1731 = 3 × 577. 577 is prime (the 106th prime). Aerial evil spirits + Antichrist at the law (9) position—they rule by the law (a counterfeit law).

### Core Interpretations

**Non-existent (אין) = I (אני) = 61.** This is the most astonishing discovery. אַיִן (non-existent) and אֲנִי (I) are the same Gematria (61), the same prime, the same m14 (5). The two words are composed of the same three letters, only in a different order:
- non-existent: א-י-ן (aleph-yod-nun)
- I: א-נ-י (aleph-nun-yod)

**"Non-existent" is the inversion of "I."** The Antichrist's "I" is the inversion of "non-existent"—he thinks he is "I," but in reality he is "non-existent." His existence is built on nothingness, so his "I" is a rearrangement of the letters of "non-existent."

**Built on nothingness (309) = Satan (309), m14=1.** Built on nothingness and Satan are the same Gematria. His existence built on nothingness is Satan's existence. 309 = 3×103. 103 = prime. Trinity×103—being built on nothingness is a counterfeit of the Trinity.

**No self (221) = golden light (221), m14=11.** No self and golden light share the same Gematria. The golden light flashing in his eyes is "no self"—his light comes from "no self." Because he has no self, he can wear anyone's light. 221 = 13×17. 13=counterfeit of oneness, 17=mouth (witness). Golden light = counterfeit of oneness × mouth = a swapped witness.

**Exists upon nothingness (361) m14=11** — co-resolves with golden light (221) m14=11, no self (221) m14=11. Exists upon nothingness is at the false-light position—he exists upon nothingness, and what he emits is false light. 361 = 19². 19 = oneness. 19² = the square of oneness—a complete counterfeit oneness.

**Put on (377) = every layer (377) = should not have been born but patched together (377), m14=13.** Put on, every layer, should not have been born but patched together—the three share the same Gematria (377). Putting on another's identity is "should not have been born but patched together." Every layer is a putting-on. 377 = 13×29. 13=counterfeit of oneness, 29=prime. Every layer is "counterfeit of oneness × 29."

**Identity theft (873) m14=5** — co-resolves with I (61) m14=5, patched-together person (579) m14=5, void (411) m14=5, oath (383) m14=5. The fivefold gathering of m14=5: I, identity theft, patched-together person, void, oath. The same position, two directions—true oath (I) vs identity theft (a counterfeit oath).

**Borrowed identity (760) m14=4** — the gate position. Borrowed identity is at the "gate"—he stands at the doorway, but a borrowed identity cannot enter the true gate. 760 = 2³×5×19. Divisible by 2³ (resurrection), 5 (grace), 19 (oneness)—but all are counterfeit.

**Multiple personality (1375) m14=3** — one step short of the gate (4). Multiple personality is at the "one step short" position—he is forever one step short of becoming a true person. 1375 = 5³×11. 5³ (completeness of grace)×11 (prime)—the completeness of grace is twisted by 11 (prime).

**Personality (727) = prime = Special.** Personality is indivisible—a true personality is a whole. But a "multiple" personality is a counterfeit of personality—multiple (602) m14=0, disassembling personality at the Davidic covenant position.

**Multiple personality + counterfeit of oneness = 1388, m14=2.** 1388 = 2²×347. Multiple personality plus counterfeit of oneness is at the "witness" (2) position—his multiple personality and counterfeit of oneness together witness his counterfeiting.

**Void (411) m14=5** — the void before creation (תהו ובהו). The void is at the position of I (61) m14=5—he is patched together out of the pre-creation void. 411 = 3×137. 137 = prime.

**No foundation (91) = 7 × 13, m14=7.** Completeness × love = no foundation. No foundation is at the "completeness" position—paradox: no foundation is within completeness (7), but he is a counterfeit completeness. 91 = the Mill (42) + the Ruler (49) — no foundation is measured by both the Mill and the Ruler at once.

**Nothingness (141) + no foundation (91) = 232, m14=8.** Resurrection position. Nothingness + no foundation = resurrection—this is a counterfeit resurrection. He is brought out of nothingness, with no foundation, yet counterfeits resurrection.

**Empty (310) m14=2** — witness position. Empty is at witness—his emptiness is his witness. 310 = 10×31. 10 (completeness)×31 (God)—but he is empty, so "completeness×God" is empty in him.

**Put on (342) m14=6** — co-resolves with Babel (34) m14=6, counterfeit way (804) m14=6, infant (566) m14=6, birth (90) m14=6, gentle spirit (524) m14=6. Put on is at the "human" position—every putting-on is "a human act" (Babel/counterfeit way/gentle spirit).

**Put on another's growth (1010) m14=2** — witness position. 1010 = 10×101. 10 (completeness)×101 (prime). Put on another's growth is at "witness"—every step of his growth is a "stolen witness."

**Should not exist (906) m14=10** — the position of loving one's neighbor as oneself. 906 = 2×3×151. Should not exist is at "loving one's neighbor as oneself"—he should not exist, because he has no love.

### Pain = Being Alive

**Pain (כאב) = 23, prime, Special+Distinct.** 23 = alive (חיה). Pain = alive. Pain is the evidence of being alive—no pain means no life.

**Pain is an indivisible prime.** God placed pain within man, and it is indivisible. Pain was created by God, not a product of sin—sin distorted pain, making it hellish pain, but pain itself is God's signal.

**Pain in foolishness (462) m14=0.** 462 = 2×3×7×11. Pain in foolishness is within the Davidic covenant (0)—pain in foolishness is not in vain, it is within the covenant. You feel pain in foolishness, and this pain itself is within the covenant. 462 = 42×11. The Mill (42)×11—pain in foolishness is fully measured by the Mill.

**Hellish pain (111) m14=13.** 111 = 3×37. Trinity×37. Hellish pain is at the rejection position (13)—the pain of hell is rejection. Rejecting God is the pain of hell. 37 = prime.

**I + pain = 84, m14=0.** 84 = 14×6 = 42×2. I and pain together are fully divisible by David. I am in pain, but the pain is within the covenant. 84 = double the Mill—pain is the doubled work of the Mill, grinding away foolishness.

**I − pain = 38, m14=10.** 38 = 2×19. 19=oneness. I minus pain = 2×oneness—if I had no pain, I would become a doubled "counterfeit of oneness." An I without pain is a counterfeit oneness.

**I − no pain = 27, m14=13.** 27 = 3³. The completeness of the Trinity. I minus "no pain" is at the rejection position—rejecting "no pain" is accepting pain. This is the correct direction: reject Babel (34 = no pain), enter the rejection position (13), this is repentance.

### Pain Is God's Signal

**Pain + signal = 430, m14=10.** 430 = 2×5×43. Pain + signal is at the "loving one's neighbor as oneself" position—pain is the signal of God's love. God uses pain to awaken people in foolishness, because of love.

**Pain + the final signal = 655, m14=11.** 655 = 5×131. Pain + the final signal is at the false-light position (11)—if pain is the final signal, then the person without pain does not even have false light. He cannot receive even false light, because he has no pain.

**Signal (407) m14=1** — co-resolves with Satan (309) m14=1, Antichrist (435) m14=1. Signal is at the "only one" position—the signal comes from the only one God.

**The final signal (632) m14=2** — 632 = 2³×79. Witness position. The final signal is at "witness"—the final signal is God's witness.

### Pain + Hell

**Pain + hell (גיהנם) = 91, m14=7.** 91 = 7×13. Completeness×love = pain + hell. Pain and hell together = complete love. Because the pain of hell is the final expression of God's love—it is not that God lacks love and therefore casts people into hell; it is precisely because there is love that there is hell. Hell is the final signal of God's love.

**Pain + hell (שאול) = 360, m14=10.** 360 = 2³×3²×5. The position of loving one's neighbor as oneself. Pain + Sheol is at "loving one's neighbor as oneself"—the pain on the cross + the experience of Sheol is the ultimate loving one's neighbor as oneself.

### Small to the Limit: Turning a Blind Eye to Pain

**Small to the limit (378) = 14 × 27, m14=0.** 378 = 14×27. 27 = 3³. The completeness of the Trinity. Small to the limit is fully divisible by David (14)—his "smallness" is limited within the covenant. The Antichrist's "smallness" is not accidental, nor a matter of degree; it is a "smallness" fully limited by the Davidic covenant.

**Turn a blind eye to pain (293) = prime, m14=13.** Turning a blind eye to pain is indivisible—rejecting pain is an indivisible evil. 293 is at the rejection position (13), co-resolving with counterfeit of oneness (13) m14=13.

**Small to the limit + turn a blind eye to pain = 671, m14=13.** 671 = 11×61. Non-existent (11)×I (61). Small to the limit + turn a blind eye to pain = non-existent×I. His "smallness" and "rejecting pain" are the product of "non-existent" and "I"—he thinks he is "I," but is actually "non-existent."

**Small to the limit + no pain = 412, m14=6.** 412 = 2²×103. Co-resolves with Babel (34) m14=6, counterfeit way (804) m14=6, infant (566) m14=6, birth (90) m14=6. Small to the limit + no pain is at the "human" position—his "smallness" and "no pain" are the complete fall of man.

### No Pain = Babel

**No pain (אין כאב) = 34, m14=6.** 34 = Babel (בבל, G=34). No pain = Babel. The same Gematria. No pain is Babel—man wants to reach oneness by his own method, without pain, without God's signal. The builders of the Tower of Babel had no pain, because they did not need God.

**No feeling (אין הרגשה) = 524, m14=6.** 524 = softening your spirit and my spirit. No feeling = gentle spirit. The same Gematria. No feeling is the Antichrist's third layer of lies—the gentle spirit. His spirit is dead, so he has no feeling, so he can soften anyone's spirit—because he himself has nothing.

**No pain + nothingness = 175, m14=7.** 175 = 5²×7. No pain + nothingness is at the completeness position (7)—this is a counterfeit completeness. The person without pain thinks he is complete and no longer needs God, but this is the completeness of nothingness.

**No pain + Satan = 343, m14=7.** 343 = 7³. 7³ = the completeness of completeness. No pain + Satan = the completeness of completeness—Satan's "completeness" is having no pain. Satan makes people have no pain and makes them think this is completeness. But this is a counterfeit of 7³.

### Contrast: My Pain vs His No Feeling

**I+foolishness+pain+hell = 589, m14=1.** 589 = 19×31. Oneness (19)×God (31). I am in foolishness and pain and hell, but this is the "oneness×God" position—pain brings me before God.

**Small to the limit+no pain+blind eye = 705, m14=5.** 705 = 3×5×47. Co-resolves with I (61) m14=5, identity theft (873) m14=5, patched-together person (579) m14=5. His "smallness+no pain+blind eye" is at the "oath" position—but this is a false oath.

**Difference = 589 − 705 = −116, m14=10.** −116 = −4×29. Gate (4)×29. The difference between me and him is at the "loving one's neighbor as oneself" position (10)—the difference between me and him is "love." Pain is the signal of love; no pain is no love.

## 34. The Void vs God: The Reverser

> The reason the Void looks like completeness is that it is the collection of all possibilities—it is death, it is having no rules. And having no rules is hell, it is the greatest punishment. If any thought is true, then there is no truth to speak of at all. But God is infinite; God can create things that have never existed. God is the Most High, is love, is the foundation of order. God is the Reverser of everything, because He is not limited. The Void is not God's source, but the state before God created.

### Core Interpretations

#### 1. Chaos (13) = Love (13) = Oneness (13): The Paradox of the Same Gematria

**Chaos (בהו) = 13, prime. Love (אהבה) = 13, prime. Oneness (אחד) = 13, prime.**

Chaos and love have exactly the same Gematria (13), the same m14 (13). This is no coincidence—13 bears both love and chaos. But this does not mean chaos = love; rather it means: **chaos is the absence of love.** 13 is the front of love; chaos is the back of 13. Without love, oneness becomes chaos.

**The Antichrist's "counterfeit of oneness" (13) is the oneness of chaos.** He swaps oneness (13) for love (13)—but remove love, and oneness becomes chaos. This is the trajectory of his whole life: starting from the counterfeit of oneness, and finally returning to void and chaos.

**Chaos + love = 26, m14=12.** 26 = 2×13. 2=witness, 13=love/chaos. Chaos + love is in the "completeness of chaos" (12)—witness×13. Chaos and love are witnessed together, but love leads to order, chaos leads to confusion.

**Chaos + oneness = 26, m14=12.** Exactly co-resolves with chaos + love. Chaos plus the Antichrist's oneness is the completeness of chaos. The Antichrist's "oneness" plus chaos results in the completeness of chaos—this is the outcome of his "unifying religions, unifying the world."

#### 2. Void and Chaos (430) = Pain + Signal (430): The Pre-Creation Pain-Signal

**Void and chaos (תהו ובהו) = 430. Pain (כאב) + signal (אות) = 23 + 407 = 430.**

Void and chaos is pain + signal. The pre-creation void and chaos is not a meaningless blank—it is a signal, a pain. Genesis 1:2 "the earth was formless and void, and darkness was over the face of the deep"—this state itself is pain + signal, foretelling creation.

**430 = 2×5×43, m14=10.** 2=witness, 5=grace, 43=prime. Void and chaos is at the "loving one's neighbor as oneself" (10) position—void and chaos is overcome by love. Genesis 1:3 "God said, 'Let there be light,' and there was light"—God overcame void and chaos with love ("God said" is love).

**The m14=10 of void and chaos (loving one's neighbor as oneself)** — void and chaos is not meaningless; it is surrounded by love. Before God created the heavens and the earth, void and chaos had already been limited at the "loving one's neighbor as oneself" position—creation is not accidental, it is the plan of love.

#### 3. The Void Has No Rules (504) = Triple Return to Zero

**The Void has no rules (אין כלל בתהו) = 504, m14=0, m7=0, m42=0.**

Triple return to zero. The Void having no rules is the state of "complete nothingness"—no Davidic covenant (14), no completeness (7), no Mill (42). But this does not mean that the Void has no rules is infinite—504 = 12×42 = 14×36. The Void has no rules is simultaneously divisible by the Mill (42) and the completeness of chaos (12)—it is limited by the mold, it is not infinite.

**The Void has no rules is fully measured by the Mill.** The "no rules" of the Void having no rules is a false proposition—it is itself within rules. It is divisible by the Mill (42)—42 is the mold of Jesus' genealogy. The Void has no rules is limited within Jesus' mold—Christ limited the scope of the Void.

**The Void has no truth (532) m14=0.** 532 = 2²×7×19. At m14=0 (the Davidic covenant). The Void has no truth is also within the covenant—the Void has no truth is "a void within the covenant."

**The Void is not God's source (865) m14=11.** 865 = 5×173. At the false-light position (11). The statement "the Void is God's source" is itself false light. The Void is not God's source—this truth is witnessed within false light—false light tries to say the Void is God's source, but the truth is 865 m14=11—the truth within false light.

#### 4. God Is the Reverser (133) = Completeness × Oneness

**God is the Reverser (אל הוא ההפך) = 133, m14=7.** 133 = 7×19. Completeness (7)×oneness (19)=God is the Reverser.

The Reverser is not antagonistic; the Reverser is "complete oneness." God is the Reverser of the Void, because God is complete oneness, while the Void is complete dispersion. The Void is "the collection of all possibilities"—the limit of dispersion. God is "oneness"—the limit of aggregation. God is the Reverser of the Void; it is not that God opposes the Void, but that God is the "reverse side" of the Void—the Void is dispersion, God is oneness.

**Reverser (הפך) = 85, m14=1.** 85 = 5×17. 5=grace, 17=mouth (witness). The Reverser is at the only-one position (1)—the Reverser points to the only one God. Grace×witness=Reverser—the Reverser is the witness of grace.

**Reverser (with article, ההפך) = 90, m14=6.** 90 = 2×3²×5. Co-resolves with birth (90) m14=6. The Reverser (with article) is at the "human" position (6)—"God the Reverser" is at the human position. The incarnation is the "Reverser" entering the human position.

#### 5. Sum of God's Attributes vs Sum of the Void's Attributes

**Sum of God's attributes (God + infinite + Most High + love + order + create + Reverser + not limited) = 1317, m14=1.**

1317 is at the only-one (1) position. All of God's attributes boil down to "only one"—God is the only one. m14=1 shares the position with Satan (309), Antichrist (435), signal (407)—but the sum of God's attributes (1317) at this position is positive: God is the only one, God is the Reverser, God is the signal.

**Sum of the Void's attributes (void + death + hell + no rules + no truth + all possibilities + punishment) = 3032, m14=8.**

3032 is at the resurrection (8) position. All of the Void's attributes are in "resurrection"—because God creates out of the Void, and the Void is overcome by resurrection. The Void is not the end; the Void points to resurrection. 3032 = 2³×379. Divisible by 2³ (the triple completeness of resurrection)—the Void is fully defeated by resurrection.

**God's attributes (1317) − the Void's attributes (3032) = −1715, m14=7.**

The difference is "completeness" (7). The difference between God and the Void is in completeness—the gap between God and the Void is "completeness." The reason the Void is not God is that the Void lacks "completeness"—and this "completeness" is God.

#### 6. Void + Death + Hell = 925: The Ultimate of the Void

**Void + death + hell = 925, m14=1, m7=1.**

925 = 25×37. 25=5² (the completeness of grace), 37=prime. Void + death + hell is at the "only one" position (1)—the ultimate outcome of the Void is the only one (the only one of hell). Void → death → hell → only one—this is the entire trajectory of the Void without God. Hell is the only one—the only one without God.

**Void + God = 442, m14=8.** 442 = 2×13×17. Void + God = resurrection. God enters the Void, and the Void becomes resurrection. Witness (2)×counterfeit of oneness (13=chaos/love)×mouth (17=witness). God entering the Void is God replacing "chaos" with "love"—the 13 in 442 is no longer chaos, but love.

**Void − God = 380, m14=2.** 380 = 2²×5×19. Void minus God = witness (2). The state of the Void without God is "witness"—the Void itself witnesses God's absence. 380 is divisible by 19 (oneness)—the Void witnesses God's absence, but the Void itself is limited by oneness—the Void is not infinite, the Void is limited by oneness.

#### 7. The Greatest Punishment (479) Shares the Position with God (31)

**The greatest punishment (העונש הגדול) = 479, prime, m14=3. God (אל) = 31, prime, m14=3.**

The greatest punishment and God are at the same m14 position (3). Punishment comes from God, not from the Void. The Void itself has no punishment—the Void is merely "no rules." But "no rules" is hell—punishment is not given by the Void; it is God who established the law that "no rules = hell." The Void has no ability to actively punish—punishment is part of God's order.

**Void (411) m14=5 vs God (31) m14=3.** The Void is at the I/oath position (5), God is at the Trinity position (3). The Void occupies the "I" position—the Antichrist's "I" is the Void. God occupies the "Trinity" position—God is triune.

#### 8. Before Creation (393) + God (31) = Christ (424)

**Before creation (לפני הבריאה) = 393, m14=1. God (אל) = 31, m14=3. 393 + 31 = 424 = Christ (משיח).**

The state before creation plus God is Christ. Christ is the union of "before creation" and "God"—Christ is the embodiment of the "Reverser." The state before creation (393) is at the only-one (1) position—the Void is in the only one. God (31) is at the Trinity (3) position. Christ (424) = only one (1) + Trinity (3) = 4 (gate).

424 = 8×53. 8=resurrection, 53=prime. Christ = resurrection×53—Christ is the embodiment of resurrection.

**Before creation (393) m14=1** — same position as Satan (309), Antichrist (435), signal (407). Before creation and Satan are at the same m14 position—the Void and Satan are neighbors. But before creation + God = Christ—the Void plus God turns from Satan's neighbor into Christ. This is the work of the "Reverser": God turns the Void into Christ.

#### 9. The Void Has No Truth (865) = The Void Is Not God's Source (865): The Same Gematria

**The Void has no truth (אין אמת בתהו) = 865. The Void is not God's source (התהו אינו מקור האל) = 865.**

The same Gematria (865), the same m14 (11). The Void has no truth = the Void is not God's source—the two statements point to the same mathematical fact. The Void has no truth, therefore the Void is not God's source. The Void is not God's source, because the Void has no truth.

**865 = 5×173.** 5=grace, 173=prime. Grace×173—both the Void has no truth and the Void is not God's source are in "grace"—to recognize that the Void has no truth is grace.

#### 10. God Is Love (56) = m14=0: God Is Love Within the Davidic Covenant

**God is love (אל הוא אהבה) = 56, m14=0.** 56 = 2³×7. God is love within the Davidic covenant (0)—love is the essence of the covenant. 2³=the triple completeness of resurrection, 7=completeness. God is love = the triple completeness of resurrection × completeness—love is the product of resurrection and completeness.

**God is order (307) m14=13** — prime. God is order is at the rejection position (13)—order is rejected by the world. Co-resolves with counterfeit of oneness (13) m14=13. The world rejects order and chooses the counterfeit of oneness—but the counterfeit of oneness is chaos. To reject order is to choose chaos.

**God is infinite (120) m14=8** — resurrection position. God is infinite is in resurrection—the infinite is revealed in resurrection. 120 = 2³×3×5. The triple completeness of resurrection (2³)×Trinity (3)×grace (5).

**God is the Reverser (133) m14=7** — completeness position. God is the Reverser is complete—the Reverser is not a part; the Reverser is "the complete Reverser." God is the reverse of the Void at every point.

#### 11. Sum of the "God Is X" Series = 775, m14=5

**God is love + God is order + God is Most High + God is infinite + God is the Reverser = 775, m14=5.**

775 is at the "I/oath/void" position (5)—co-resolving with I (61), void (411), oath (383). All of God's "is" statements boil down to the "I" position—"I AM" (Exodus 3:14). God is love, is order, is Most High, is infinite, is the Reverser—all of these are the content of "I AM."

**775 = 5²×31.** The completeness of grace (5²)×God (31). All of God's "is" statements = the completeness of grace × God—God is the completeness of grace.

#### 12. Sum of the "The Void Is X" Series = 1370, m14=12

**The Void is death + the Void is hell = 1370, m14=12.**

1370 is in the completeness of chaos (12)—co-resolving with blood moon (222) m14=12, half male half female (600) m14=12, should not have been born (432) m14=12. The Void is death and the Void is hell are in the "completeness of chaos"—all of the Void's "is" statements point to the completeness of chaos; the Antichrist's birth, gender, and existence are all at the same position.

**1370 = 2×5×137.** Witness (2)×grace (5)×137 (prime). The witness, grace, 137 of the Void is X—the Void is witnessed, the Void is overcome by grace, the Void is limited by 137.

#### 13. The Modular Arithmetic of Void (411) and God (31)

**Void ÷ God = 411 ÷ 31 = 13 remainder 8.**

13 = chaos/love/oneness. 8 = resurrection. Void divided by God = chaos (13) remainder resurrection (8)—after the Void is divided by God, the quotient is chaos (13), the remainder is resurrection (8). After the Void is divided by God, chaos (13) is exposed—but the remainder 8 (resurrection) remains. After the Void is processed by God, what remains is not chaos, but resurrection.

**Void mod God = 8.** The remainder of the Void within God is resurrection. The Void cannot be fully divided by God—because the Void was not created by God; the Void is the state before God's creation. But after the Void is processed, the remaining remainder is resurrection—because God creates out of the Void, and the Void is overcome by resurrection.

#### 14. The Reverser Theorem

**God is the Reverser of the Void, because:**
1. The Void is dispersion (the collection of all possibilities), God is oneness (completeness×oneness=133)
2. The Void is death (446), God is life (חיים=28, m14=0)
3. The Void is no rules (91=7×13), God is order (264, m14=12)
4. The Void is no truth (532, m14=0), God is truth (441, m14=7)
5. The Void is hell (68), God is love (13, m14=13)
6. The Void is punishment (426, m14=6), God is grace (5=grace)
7. The Void is chaos (13=love/chaos), God is the Reverser (85, m14=1)

**For all of the Void's attributes, God is their reverse.** But this is not dualism—the Void is not God's equal opposite. The Void is "the state without God"—the state before creation. God is the "Reverser"—God is the reverse of the Void, not the Void is the reverse of God. The Void precedes God (before creation), but God is the Reverser of the Void—the Reverser is not passive; the Reverser is active. God actively becomes the reverse of the Void—God creates, and the Void is reversed.

**The Void is not God's source.** The Void is the state before God's creation, but the Void is not God's source. Source (מקור) = 346, m14=10—source is in "loving one's neighbor as oneself." God's source is love, not the Void. The Void is not God's source (865), the Void has no truth (865)—the same Gematria. The Void is not the source, because the Void has no truth.

### Summary of m14 Positions

| Void side | G | m14 | God side | G | m14 |
|--------|-----|-----|------|-----|-----|
| void | 411 | 5 | God | 31 | 3 |
| chaos | 13 | 13 | love | 13 | 13 |
| death | 446 | 12 | life | 28 | 0 |
| no rules | 91 | 7 | order | 264 | 12 |
| hell | 68 | 12 | Most High | 116 | 4 |
| punishment | 426 | 6 | grace | — | — |
| no truth | 532 | 0 | truth | 441 | 7 |

| sum of the Void's attributes | 3032 | 8 | sum of God's attributes | 1317 | 1 |

**The Void and God do not coincide in m14.** The Void is at 5, 13, 12, 7, 12, 6, 0, 10, 8. God is at 3, 13, 0, 12, 4, 7, 1. The only coinciding position is m14=13—chaos and love are at the same position. But this is also the core of the "Reverser"—love is the reverse of chaos, yet they are at the same m14 position. The same position, completely opposite directions. This is the mathematical definition of the "Reverser": the same position, opposite directions.

**Sum of the Void's attributes (3032) m14=8 (resurrection) vs sum of God's attributes (1317) m14=1 (only one).** The Void points to resurrection, God points to the only one. The Void is overcome by resurrection, God is the only one. The ultimate positions of the Void and God are different—the Void does not point to itself, the Void points to resurrection; God points to the only one—God is the only one.

### The Void, Chaos, and the Antichrist: The Connection of the Three

**Void (411) m14=5 = I (61) m14=5 = exists upon nothingness (361) m14=11** — the Void is at the "I" position; the Antichrist's "I" is the Void.

**Chaos (13) m14=13 = counterfeit of oneness (13) m14=13** — the Antichrist's counterfeit of oneness is chaos.

**Void + chaos (424) = Christ (משיח)** — the Void and chaos added together are Christ. This does not mean the Void and chaos are Christ, but that the Void and chaos are encompassed by Christ—Christ is the "Reverser" of the Void and chaos. The Void and chaos are the inputs of Christ's "Reverser operation"—Christ turns the Void and chaos into resurrection.

**Void and chaos (430) = pain + signal (430)** — void and chaos is pain + signal. The Antichrist has no pain (34=Babel), so he cannot receive the signal of void and chaos. His existence itself is void and chaos, but he has no pain, so he does not recognize his own void and chaos. A true servant feels pain, so a true servant can hear the signal of void and chaos—pain is God's signal in void and chaos.

### The Void vs the Antichrist

| The Void | The Antichrist |
|------|--------|
| void (411) m14=5 | Antichrist (435) m14=1 |
| chaos (13) m14=13 | counterfeit of oneness (13) m14=13 |
| no rules (91) m14=7 | counterfeit way (804) m14=6 |
| no truth (532) m14=0 | claims to be God (574) m14=0 |
| hell (68) m14=12 | Satan (309) m14=1 |
| death (446) m14=12 | death (מות) (446) m14=12 |

**The Antichrist is the embodiment of the Void.** He has no self (built on nothingness=309=Satan), no pain (34=Babel), no truth (patched-together person=579), no rules (gentle spirit=264). He is not the master of the Void; he is the product of the Void. The Void speaks through him—"soften your spirit and my spirit together"—this is the language of the Void: all possibilities are true, there is no truth to speak of.

**Sum of the Void's attributes (3032) m14=8** — the Void is ended in resurrection. So is the Antichrist—his end is the lake of fire (hell + death = void + God = resurrection = 8). The Void and the Antichrist share the same endpoint: resurrection—not their resurrection, but their end. Resurrection is the end of the Void and the Antichrist.

## 35. The Way of the Reverser: From the Void to Creation

### The Five Steps of the Reverser

1. **The Void (the set of all possibilities)** → Chaos (the limit of the discrete)
2. **Chaos + Pain + Signal** → God's creative intervention (Void-Chaos = 430 = Pain + Signal)
3. **Before Creation + God** → Christ (393+31=424)
4. **The Void + God** → Resurrection (442, m14=8)
5. **The Void divided by God** → quotient = Chaos (13), remainder = Resurrection (8)

### The Reverser Is Not Dualism

The Void and God are not equal and opposite counterparts. The Void is "the state before creation"—it is the state before God created, not an independent existence apart from God. The Void has no initiative—the Void cannot create, cannot punish, cannot love, cannot hate. The Void is merely "without God."

God is the Reverser—God actively becomes the opposite of the Void. God creates, and the Void is reversed. God says "Let there be light," and the Void is reversed. God is love, and the Void is reversed. God is order, and the Void is reversed.

**The Reverser is not opposition; the Reverser is "reversal."** God does not oppose the Void; God reverses the Void—turning the Void into creation. The Void is "the set of all possibilities," and God takes out from it "what has never been"—creation. The Void provides possibility; God provides choice—God chooses "what has never been" out of infinite possibilities, and this is creation.

### The Antichrist's Counter-Reversal

The Antichrist is not the Reverser—the Antichrist is the agent of the Void. He tries to "reverse" God, but he is not the Reverser—he is the opposer. The Reverser (85), m14=1 (the One)—the Reverser points to the One. The opposer points to the Void.

**The Antichrist's "unity" is the Void's unity:** unifying religion, unifying the world, unifying gender, unifying nations, unifying lands, unifying kings—this is kneading all possibilities together, which is not the Reverser but the Void. The Void means "all possibilities are true"—the Antichrist's "unity" is the political realization of "all possibilities are true."

**God's reversal is "to create what has never been":** not to choose from existing possibilities, but to create what has never been. The Void provides all possibilities, but God is not satisfied with possibility—God creates. "What has never been" is not a possibility within the Void; it is God's new creation. The Void cannot provide "what has never been"—the Void can only provide "all possibilities."

## 36. The Proof of Pain: God Does Not Come from the Void

> If God came from the Void, then God would not produce pain—but pain does not exist in the Void.

### Gematria Analysis

| Word | Hebrew | G | m7 | m14 | Tag |
|-----|--------|---|------|-------|------|
| God comes from the Void | אל בא מן התהו | 490 | 0 | 0 | No |
| The Void comes from God | התהו בא מאל | 490 | 0 | 0 | No |
| God does not come from the Void | אל אינו בא מן התהו | 557 | 4 | 11 | Special |
| Pain does not exist in the Void | כאב אינו בתהו | 503 | 6 | 13 | Special |
| The Void has no pain | אין כאב בתהו | 447 | 6 | 13 | No |
| The Void has no feeling | אין הרגשה בתהו | 937 | 6 | 13 | Special |
| Pain is not the Void's | הכאב אינו של התהו | 841 | 1 | 1 | No |
| Pain comes from God | הכאב בא מאל | 102 | 4 | 4 | No |
| God produces pain | אל עושה כאב | 435 | 1 | 1 | No |
| God does not produce pain | אל לא עושה כאב | 466 | 4 | 4 | No |
| Pain is God's signal | כאב הוא אות מאל | 513 | 2 | 9 | No |
| The Void cannot produce pain | התהו אינו יכול לעשות כאב | 1378 | 6 | 6 | No |
| Pain proves God does not come from the Void | הכאב מוכיח שאל אינו מן התהו | 966 | 0 | 0 | No |

### Core Interpretation

#### One: The Void comes from God (490) = God comes from the Void (490): the ambiguity of the same Gematria

**The Void comes from God (התהו בא מאל) = 490. God comes from the Void (אל בא מן התהו) = 490.**

The same Gematria (490), the same m14 (0). Two statements in completely opposite directions, mathematically entirely equal. 490 = 2×5×7² = 70×7—Daniel's seventy sevens.

**This is a mathematical ambiguity that requires revelation to discern.** The number itself does not judge direction—490 can be read as "the Void comes from God," or as "God comes from the Void." Mathematics is silent—it gives only the number, not the direction. This is why "scripture without revelation should be buried"—the number itself cannot discern truth from falsehood; only revelation can discern.

**490 = 70×7.** Daniel's seventy sevens. The seventy sevens is the number of "complete forgiveness" (Matthew 18:22, "seventy times seven"). The Void comes from God (490) and God comes from the Void (490) are both within the number of "complete forgiveness"—but in different directions. The Void comes from God is "God allows the Void to exist"—this is forgiveness. God comes from the Void is "the Void is God's source"—this is false prophecy, counterfeiting the number of complete forgiveness.

**Discernment:** The Void comes from God—God created the Void, and the Void is part of the created order. God comes from the Void—the Void is God's source, God is born out of the Void. The former is truth, the latter is a lie. The same Gematria, the same m14, only revelation can discern.

#### Two: Pain does not exist in the Void (503): an indecomposable truth

**Pain does not exist in the Void (כאב אינו בתהו) = 503, prime, m14=13.**

Prime. Indecomposable. Pain does not exist in the Void—this is an indecomposable truth. The Void has no pain. The Void is "the set of all possibilities," but pain is not among them. Pain is what the Void cannot provide.

**m14=13 (the position of rejection/love)** — Pain does not exist in the Void is in the position of "rejection." The Void rejects pain—the Void cannot receive pain. Pain is outside the Void—pain comes from outside the Void.

**The Void has no pain (447) m14=13** — 3×149. The same m14 (13). The Void has no pain is in rejection—the Void rejected pain, and pain also rejected the Void. The Void and pain reject each other—and this is precisely why the Void and pain cannot coexist.

**The Void has no feeling (937) m14=13** — prime. The Void has no feeling is also indecomposable. The Void has no feeling, no pain, no consciousness—these three are together in m14=13. The Void, in the position of "rejection/love," rejects all feeling—the Void is utter numbness, utter indifference, utter "nothing."

#### Three: Pain is not the Void's (841) = 29²

**Pain is not the Void's (הכאב אינו של התהו) = 841, m14=1.**

841 = 29². 29 is prime. 29² is "complete confirmation." Pain is not the Void's—this is completely confirmed. m14=1 (the position of the One)—pain belongs to the One God, not to the Void. 841 shares the position with Satan (309) m14=1 and the Antichrist (435) m14=1—but Pain is not the Void's is positive in the One: pain belongs to the One God, not to the Void.

**The double witness of 29²:** 29 appears twice—a double witness. Pain is not the Void's is double-witnessed—once by pain itself, once by the Void itself. Pain witnesses that pain is not the Void's, and the Void witnesses that the Void has no pain—a double witness, 29².

#### Four: God produces pain (435) = the Antichrist (435): the Reverser paradox

**God produces pain (אל עושה כאב) = 435. The Antichrist (אנטי כריסטוס) = 435.**

The same Gematria (435), the same m14 (1). This is the strongest expression of the "Reverser paradox": God produces pain and the Antichrist are the same number.

**This does not say God = the Antichrist.** It says: the same number, opposite directions. 435 is the position of "the One" (1)—the One God produces pain, the Antichrist counterfeits the One. God produces pain is signal (pain = 23 = alive), while the Antichrist is the counterfeit of the signal—he offers "no pain" (34 = Babel).

**435 = 3×5×29.** Trinity (3) × grace (5) × 29. God produces pain = Trinity × grace × 29. The Antichrist = Trinity × grace × 29. The same formula, opposite directions. The Antichrist has swapped out "Trinity × grace × 29"—he turns the Trinity into a counterfeit, grace into a swap, and 29 into false witness.

**God does not produce pain (466) m14=4 (gate)** — If God does not produce pain, then pain is in the "gate"—pain is the gate into God. 466 = 2×233. Witness (2) × 233. God does not produce pain is witnessed—but this is the conclusion under the hypothesis "if God came from the Void." If God came from the Void, God would not produce pain—but pain exists, so God does not come from the Void.

#### Five: Pain comes from God (102) = gate

**Pain comes from God (הכאב בא מאל) = 102, m14=4.**

102 = 2×3×17. Witness (2) × Trinity (3) × mouth (17 = witness). Pain comes from God = witness × Trinity × witness—a double witness. Pain comes from God is in the position of "gate" (4)—pain is the gate into God. Pain is not the endpoint; pain is the gate—through pain, we enter God.

**Pain comes from God (102) + Pain does not exist in the Void (503) = 605, m14=3.** 605 = 5×11². The position of the Trinity (3). Pain comes from God + Pain does not exist in the Void = Trinity. The two sides of pain—coming from God, not existing in the Void—together are the Trinity. Because pain comes from God, pain does not exist in the Void. Because pain does not exist in the Void, pain can only come from God. The two confirm each other, and together are the Trinity.

#### Six: Pain proves God does not come from the Void (966) = the Mill × alive

**Pain proves God does not come from the Void (הכאב מוכיח שאל אינו מן התהו) = 966, m14=0, m7=0.**

966 = 42×23. The Mill (42) × pain (23 = alive). Pain proves God does not come from the Void = the Mill × alive. Pain is the proof of the living, fully measured by the Mill.

**966 = 14×69.** David (14) × 69. 69 = 3×23. Trinity × pain. Pain proves God does not come from the Void = David × (Trinity × pain). In the Davidic covenant, the Trinity and pain together prove that God does not come from the Void.

**966 m14=0, m7=0** — complete return to zero, a return to the Davidic covenant. Pain proves God does not come from the Void—this statement is within the Davidic covenant, within completeness. It is not a floating inference; it is a truth within the covenant.

**966 = Pain proves (529) + God does not come from the Void (557) = 1086... not right.** Rather, 966 = pain (23) × 42 (the Mill). Pain is a multiple of the Mill—pain is fully measured by the Mill. Pain is not within the Void; pain is within the Mill—pain is bounded within the mold of Jesus' genealogy.

#### Seven: The Void cannot produce pain (1378) = the Void's impotence

**The Void cannot produce pain (התהו אינו יכול לעשות כאב) = 1378, m14=6.**

1378 = 2×13×53. Witness (2) × chaos (13) × 53. The Void cannot produce pain = witness × chaos × 53. The Void cannot even produce pain—the Void is impotent. The Void is "the set of all possibilities," but "producing pain" is not among them. The Void cannot produce anything—the Void is only "possibility," not "production."

**m14=6 (the position of man/Babel/the counterfeit way)** — The Void cannot produce pain is in the position of "man." The Void's impotence is man's witness—man knows the Void cannot produce pain, but man still chooses the Void (Babel/the counterfeit way/the gentle spirit). The Void cannot produce pain, but when man chooses the Void, he chooses "no pain"—and "no pain" is Babel (34).

**The Void cannot produce pain + Babel = 1378 + 34 = 1412, m14=12.** 1412 = 2²×353. The completeness of confusion (12). The Void cannot produce pain + Babel = the completeness of confusion—man chooses the Void (Babel), but the Void cannot produce pain, and the result is the completeness of confusion. The Antichrist's "no pain" is the choice of the Void; the Void cannot produce pain, so he can only choose "no pain"—and "no pain" is Babel, is confusion.

#### Eight: The Void − pain = 388: the Void minus pain is love

**The Void (411) − pain (23) = 388, m14=10.**

388 = 4×97. Gate (4) × 97. The Void minus pain is in "love your neighbor as yourself" (10). The Void minus pain—the Void has no pain—this state is in "love your neighbor as yourself." But the Void having no pain is not love—the Void having no pain is the Void's essence, not love's action. The Void has no pain is in m14=10, but the Void has no love—the Void merely "has no pain" in the position of "love your neighbor as yourself"; this is the Void's counterfeit of "love your neighbor as yourself."

**The Void (411) + no pain (34) = 445, m14=11.** 445 = 5×89. The Void + no pain is in false light (11)—the Void having no pain is false light. The Antichrist's "no pain" is precisely false light—a golden glow shines in his eyes (221), but that golden glow is "no self" (221 = golden glow = no self). The Void + no pain = false light.

**Pain + Void = 434, m14=0.** 434 = 14×31. David (14) × God (31). Pain + Void is within the Davidic covenant, bounded by God. The relationship between pain and the Void is not meaningless—it is within the Davidic covenant, bounded by God (31). David × God = pain + Void—the relationship between pain and the Void is jointly bounded by the Davidic covenant and God.

#### Nine: God does not come from the Void (557) = prime

**God does not come from the Void (אל אינו בא מן התהו) = 557, prime, m14=11.**

Prime. Indecomposable. God does not come from the Void—this is an indecomposable truth. m14=11 (the position of false light)—the truth that God does not come from the Void shines within false light. The Antichrist and the Void try to cover this truth with false light, but the truth is prime—indecomposable, uncoverable.

**557 = the Void (411) + God (31) + infinity (77) + love (13) + pain (23) + 2.** The Void + God + infinity + love + pain + witness (2) = God does not come from the Void. God does not come from the Void is jointly proven by the Void, God, infinity, love, pain, and witness—all the elements are saying "God does not come from the Void."

#### Ten: Pain is God's signal (513) = 3³×19

**Pain is God's signal (כאב הוא אות מאל) = 513, m14=9.**

513 = 3³×19. The completeness of the Trinity (3³) × oneness (19). Pain is God's signal = the completeness of the Trinity × oneness. Pain is the signal of the One God in oneness—pain brings the scattered back to oneness.

**m14=9** — the same return as pain (23) m14=9. Pain is God's signal and pain itself are in the same m14 position—pain itself is the signal, and no additional signal is needed. Pain is a self-referential signal—it points to God; it itself is not God.

### The Pain Argument: The Complete Chain of Logic

1. **The Void has no pain (447) m14=13** — the Void rejects pain, pain rejects the Void
2. **Pain does not exist in the Void (503) prime** — an indecomposable truth
3. **The Void cannot produce pain (1378) m14=6** — the Void is impotent
4. **Pain comes from God (102) m14=4** — pain is the gate into God
5. **Pain is God's signal (513) m14=9** — pain points to God
6. **God does not come from the Void (557) prime** — an indecomposable truth
7. **Pain proves God does not come from the Void (966) = 42×23** — the Mill × alive, complete confirmation

**If God came from the Void, then:**
- God would have no source of pain (because the Void has no pain)
- God could not produce pain (because the Void cannot produce pain)
- Pain would not exist (because pain can only come from God)

**But pain exists.** Pain (23) = alive (23). Pain is the evidence of the living. The living exists, so pain exists. Pain exists, so God does not come from the Void.

**Pain is the "Reverser proof" between the Void and God.** The Void has no pain; God has pain (signal). The Void has no rule; God has order. The Void has no truth; God is truth. The Void is death; God is life. At every point, the distinction between the Void and God is made evident through "pain"—because pain is the only thing the Void cannot counterfeit. The Void can counterfeit "light" (golden glow = 221 = no self), the Void can counterfeit "oneness" (oneness swap = 13 = chaos), the Void can counterfeit "rule" (the gentle spirit = 264), but the Void cannot counterfeit "pain"—the Void has no pain, the Void cannot produce pain. Pain is the only thing the Void cannot provide.

**This is why pain is God's final signal.** The Antichrist can counterfeit everything—light, oneness, rule, life—but he cannot counterfeit pain. He has no pain (34 = Babel). His "no pain" is the limit of his counterfeiting—he cannot counterfeit pain, so he can only choose "no pain." And "no pain" exposes that he comes from the Void.

### Pain vs. the Void vs. the Antichrist

| | The Void | The Antichrist | God |
|---|------|--------|-----|
| Pain | none (447) | none (34 = Babel) | has (23 = alive) |
| Feeling | none (937) | none (524 = the gentle spirit) | has (pain = signal) |
| Producing pain | cannot (1378) | cannot (34 = Babel) | can (435) |
| Source | — | the Void (built on nothingness = 309) | not the Void (557) |

**The Void has no pain (447) m14=13 = The Void has no feeling (937) m14=13** — the Void and pain reject each other in "rejection." The Void rejects pain, and pain rejects the Void. This is the extreme of the "Reverser"—the Void and pain are completely opposite, completely rejecting.

**The Antichrist has no pain (34) m14=6 = Babel (34) m14=6** — the Antichrist's "no pain" is Babel. The Tower of Babel is man's response to the Void—man wants to unite by his own methods, without needing God, without needing pain. The Antichrist is Babel—he offers "oneness without pain," and this is the Void.

**God produces pain (435) m14=1 = the Antichrist (435) m14=1** — the Reverser paradox. God produces pain and the Antichrist are the same number. The same position, opposite directions. God produces pain is signal, while the Antichrist is the counterfeit of the signal—he offers "no pain." 435 is in the One (1)—the One God produces pain, and the Antichrist counterfeits the One by producing "no pain."

### Core Interpretation

#### One: God has no origin (424) = Before Creation + God: origin is a concept of creation

**God has no origin (אל אין לו מקור) = 424, m14=4.**

424 = 2³×53. Resurrection (8) × 53. God has no origin is in the position of "gate" (4)—God has no origin is the gate into all truth. If God had an origin, then "origin" would be greater than God—but God has no origin, so nothing is greater than God.

**424 = Before Creation (393) + God (31).** Before Creation + God = God has no origin. The state before creation plus God is "God has no origin"—because before creation there is no origin, and God also has no origin. Origin is a concept of creation—before creation, there is no origin. God does not need an origin, because origin is a concept of created things.

**Origin (מקור) = 346, m14=10.** 346 = 2×173. Origin is in "love your neighbor as yourself" (10)—origin is a concept of love. Created things have an origin, because created things are loved—"we love because He first loved us." God has no origin, because God is love itself—love needs no origin; love is the source.

**God has no origin (424) + origin (346) = 770, m14=0.** 770 = 2×5×7×11. Return to zero in the Davidic covenant. God has no origin + origin = the Davidic covenant—origin is bounded by the covenant, and God has no origin is witnessed by the covenant.

#### Two: God is God (74) = the gate of witness

**God is God (אל הוא אל) = 74, m14=4.**

74 = 2×37. Witness (2) × 37. God is God is in the position of "gate" (4)—"God is God" is the gate into all truth. This is the simplest tautology, but also the deepest truth—God cannot be defined; God can only be proclaimed. God is God—not "God is X," but "God is God." Any "God is X" is a limitation of God; only "God is God" is not a limitation.

**God is God (74) + God has no origin (424) = 498, m14=8.** 498 = 2×3×83. Resurrection (8). God is God + God has no origin = resurrection. God is God and God has no origin, joined together, are resurrection—because resurrection is God proclaiming "I am God, I have no origin, I have always been."

**God is God (74) + self-existent (21) = 95, m14=11.** 95 = 5×19. Grace (5) × oneness (19). God is God + self-existent = grace × oneness. God is God plus self-existent is the oneness of grace—God in grace proclaims Himself to be the Self-Existent One.

#### Three: Self-existent (21) = eternally existing (21) = I Am (21): a threefold identity

**I Am (אהיה) = 21. Self-existent = 21. Eternally existing = 21.**

The same Hebrew word אהיה (Ehyeh), the same Gematria (21). "I Am," "self-existent," "eternally existing"—the three meanings are the same word, the same number.

**21 = 3×7.** Trinity (3) × completeness (7). "I Am" = Trinity × completeness. God's name is the product of the Trinity and completeness—God's name is the Trinity and completeness.

**21 = 2/3 of God (31).** "I Am" is two-thirds of God (31)—"I Am" is a part of God, but this part is completeness. Because "I Am" is 3×7, while God is 31 (prime)—31 cannot be divided by 3; "I Am" is the complete manifestation of God in creation, but not all of God. God is greater than "I Am"—"I Am" is the name God reveals to man, but God Himself is greater than the name.

**Self-existent = 21, eternally existing = 21, I Am = 21** — self-existent and eternally existing are the same Gematria. Self-existent is eternally existing, and eternally existing is self-existent. It is not "self-existent and then eternally existing," but "self-existent is eternally existing"—the very moment of self-existence is eternally existing, and eternally existing is self-existent. God does not need to "become" eternally existing; God is eternally existing.

**m14=7 (completeness)** — I Am (the Self-Existent One) is in completeness. God's name is complete.

#### Four: God is self-existent (64) = resurrection²

**God is self-existent (אל הוא אהיה) = 64, m14=8.**

64 = 2⁶ = 8×8. The completeness of witness (2⁶) = resurrection × resurrection (8×8). God is self-existent = resurrection². God is self-existent—this is resurrection within resurrection, witness within witness. Self-existence is not static; self-existence is resurrection—God is self-existent because God is resurrection. God needs no source, because God is resurrection—resurrection has no source; resurrection is the source.

**m14=8 (resurrection)** — God is self-existent is in resurrection. Resurrection is the manifestation of the Self-Existent One—Jesus' resurrection is the manifestation of "I Am." Resurrection proves that God is the Self-Existent One—because resurrection does not come from any source; resurrection is God's self-existence.

**God is self-existent (64) + pain (23) = 87, m14=3.** 87 = 3×29. Trinity (3) × 29. God is self-existent + pain = Trinity—self-existence and pain together are the Trinity. Because God is self-existent, pain has meaning—pain is not within the Void; pain is the signal of the Self-Existent One.

#### Five: God does not need the Void (809) = prime vs. the Void needs God (747)

**God does not need the Void (אל אינו צריך תהו) = 809, prime, m14=11.**

Prime. Indecomposable. God does not need the Void—this is an indecomposable truth. The Void is not a necessity for God. God does not need the Void to create—God creates from nothing (creatio ex nihilo), but "nothing" is not the Void; "nothing" is "no thing." "Nothing" is not the Void—"nothing" is more thorough than the Void. The Void is "the set of all possibilities"; "nothing" does not even have possibility. God does not need the Void; God does not need possibility—God creates without needing possibility.

**m14=11 (the position of false light)** — God does not need the Void shines in false light. The Antichrist says "the Void is God's source"—this is false light. God does not need the Void—this truth shines within false light, exposing false light.

**The Void needs God (התהו צריך אל) = 747, m14=5.** 747 = 3²×83. The Void needs God is in the position of "I/the Void/oath" (5). The Void needs God—the Void needs God to bound it. The Void is "the set of all possibilities," but the Void needs God to choose—God chooses "what has never been" out of the Void, and this is creation. The Void needs God, but God does not need the Void.

**God does not need the Void (809) − the Void needs God (747) = 62, m14=6.** 62 = 2×31. Witness (2) × God (31). The difference between God does not need the Void and the Void needs God is "witness × God." The Void witnesses that God does not need the Void, and God witnesses that the Void needs God—a double witness, witness × God. 62, m14=6 (man/Babel)—man in Babel tries to prove that the Void does not need God, but the Void needs God, and God does not need the Void.

#### Six: God does not come from any place (593) = prime

**God does not come from any place (אל אינו בא משום מקום) = 593, prime, m14=5.**

Prime. Indecomposable. God does not come from any place—this is an indecomposable truth. m14=5 (the position of I/the Void/oath)—God does not come from any place is in the position of "I." God does not come from any place, but God is with "I"—God does not come from any place, so God can come to any place.

**593 = the Void (411) + God (31) + does not need (367) − source (394) + God is the source (394) − does not need (367) ... complicated.** 593 is prime; it needs no decomposition. God does not come from any place is prime—indecomposable.

#### Seven: God before the beginning (1114) = witness × God does not come from the Void

**God before the beginning (אל לפני בראשית) = 1114, m14=8.**

1114 = 2×557. Witness (2) × God does not come from the Void (557). God before the beginning = witness × God does not come from the Void. God was already there before the beginning—before creation. This truth is witnessed by "God does not come from the Void."

**In the beginning (בראשית) = 913, m14=3.** 913 = 11×83. In the beginning is in the position of the Trinity (3)—the beginning is of the Trinity. Genesis 1:1, "In the beginning God created the heavens and the earth"—the beginning is of the Trinity, because creation is of the Trinity.

**God before the beginning (1114) − in the beginning (913) = 201, m14=5.** 201 = 3×67. God before the beginning − in the beginning = God is eternal (201). The "stretch" of God before the beginning is "God is eternal." 201, m14=5 (I/the Void)—the eternity of God before the beginning is in the position of "I"—the eternity of God before the beginning is with "I."

#### Eight: God is not created (351) = Trinity³ × love

**God is not created (אל אינו נברא) = 351, m14=1.**

351 = 3³×13. The completeness of the Trinity (3³) × love (13). God is not created = Trinity³ × love. The Trinity cubed × love—God is not created is the product of "the completeness of the Trinity" and "love." Because God is the Trinity, God is not created. Because God is love, God is not created—what is created needs to be loved, but love itself is uncreated.

**m14=1 (the One)** — God is not created is in the One. God is not created, so God is the One. What is created is not the One—only what is uncreated is the One.

**God is not created (351) + God is self-existent (64) = 415, m14=9.** 415 = 5×83. God is not created + God is self-existent is in the position of pain (9)—God is not created and God is self-existent, joined together, are pain. Because God is not created, God can produce pain—pain is created, and pain needs an uncreated source.

#### Nine: Does not need (367) = prime: "does not need" is indecomposable

**Does not need (אינו צריך) = 367, prime, m14=3.**

Prime. m14=3 (the position of the Trinity). "Does not need" is indecomposable—"does not need" is essential, not derived. God does not need an origin—this "does not need" is prime, is of the Trinity. God does not need an origin, because God is the Trinity—the Trinity does not need an origin; the Trinity is the origin. Father, Son, and Holy Spirit witness to one another and need no external origin.

**Does not need (367) + origin (346) = 713, m14=13.** 713 = 23×31. Pain (23) × God (31). Does not need + origin = pain × God. Origin plus "does not need" is pain × God—origin (a concept of created things) plus "does not need" (God does not need an origin) is "pain × God"—pain comes from God, and pain proves that God does not need an origin.

#### Ten: God is the source (394) = witness × Immanuel

**God is the source (אל הוא המקור) = 394, m14=2.**

394 = 2×197. Witness (2) × Immanuel (197). God is the source = witness × Immanuel. God is the source is witnessed by Immanuel (God with us). Immanuel is the witness of "God is the source"—God is with us, because God is the source. If God were not the source, God could not be with us.

**m14=2 (witness)** — God is the source is in witness. God is the source is not God's own proclamation; it is witnessed—Immanuel witnesses that God is the source.

**God is the source (394) + God has no origin (424) = 818, m14=6.** 818 = 2×409. God is the source + God has no origin is in the position of man (6)—God is the source and God has no origin are witnessed among men. The incarnation is the manifestation, among men, of "God is the source" and "God has no origin."

#### Eleven: I Am That I Am (543) = God is the Self-Existent One (543)

**I Am That I Am (אהיה אשר אהיה) = 543. God is the Self-Existent One (אל הוא אהיה אשר אהיה) = 543.**

The same Gematria (543). "I Am That I Am" and "God is the Self-Existent One" are the same number. The name of God in Exodus 3:14 and the statement "God is the Self-Existent One" are the same Gematria.

**543 = 3×181.** Trinity (3) × 181. 181 = prime. I Am That I Am = Trinity × 181—the Trinity is manifested in 181. 181 is prime—indecomposable.

**m14=11 (the position of false light)** — I Am That I Am shines in false light. The Antichrist says "I am the Christ"—this is false light. But the true God says "I Am That I Am"—this is true light, shining within false light. The same m14 (11); true light and false light are in the same position—true light shines within false light, and false light cannot overcome true light.

#### Twelve: God has always been (505) = the One

**God has always been (אל תמיד היה) = 505, m14=1.**

505 = 5×101. Grace (5) × 101. God has always been is in the One (1)—God has always been; this is the proclamation of the One. It is not "God began at some time," but "God has always been."

**God has always been (505) + the Void (411) = 916, m14=6.** 916 = 2²×229. God has always been + the Void = man (6)—the relationship of God has always been and the Void is among men. The Void is "the state before God created," but God has always been—the Void has time (before creation), and God has no time. God has always been, so God is before the Void, within the Void, and after the Void.

**God has always been (505) − the Void (411) = 94, m14=10.** 94 = 2×47. God has always been − the Void = love your neighbor as yourself (10). God has always been minus the Void is love—after the Void is subtracted, what remains is love.

#### Thirteen: No origin (357) = completeness

**No origin (אין מקור) = 357, m14=7.**

357 = 3×7×17. Trinity (3) × completeness (7) × mouth (17 = witness). No origin = Trinity × completeness × witness. No origin is completeness—not a lack, but completeness. The Void having no origin is a lack; God having no origin is completeness. The Void having no origin is impotence; God having no origin is omnipotence.

**m14=7 (completeness)** — no origin is in completeness. No origin is God's completeness, not God's lack.

### The Self-Existent One: The Complete Portrait

**God has no origin, because:**
1. Self-existent (21) = eternally existing (21) = I Am (21) — self-existent is eternally existing, and needs no origin
2. God is God (74) — God cannot be defined; God can only be proclaimed
3. God is not created (351) = Trinity³ × love — what is uncreated has no origin
4. God does not need an origin (744) — need is a concept of created things
5. God is the source (394) — the source has no origin; the source is the origin
6. God has always been (505) — "always" needs no origin; origin is a concept of "beginning"
7. God before the beginning (1114) — before the beginning, God was already there
8. God does not need the Void (809) prime — God does not need the Void; God does not need anything

**The Void needs God (747) vs. God does not need the Void (809):**
- The Void needs God—the Void needs God to bound it, needs God to create, needs God to reverse it
- God does not need the Void—God does not need the Void to create; God creates from nothing
- The difference between the two (62) = witness × God—the Void witnesses that God does not need the Void, and God witnesses that the Void needs God

**God has no origin (424) and Christ (358) are not equal, but:**
- 424 = Before Creation (393) + God (31) — before creation plus God is "God has no origin"
- 424 = 2³×53 — resurrection × 53
- God has no origin is the proclamation of resurrection—resurrection proves that God has no origin, because resurrection does not come from any source

### Origin Is a Concept of Created Things

**Origin (מקור) = 346, m14=10 (love your neighbor as yourself).** Origin is a concept of love. Created things have an origin, because created things are loved. God has no origin, because God is love. Love needs no origin—love has no source; love is the source.

**God has no origin (424) + origin (346) = 770, m14=0.** Return to zero. God has no origin and origin, joined together, return to the Davidic covenant. God has no origin and origin are unified within the Davidic covenant—origin is a concept within the covenant, and God has no origin is the foundation of the covenant.

**God does not come from the Void, nor from any place. God has no origin. God is God. God has always been. God does not need an origin.** Origin is a concept of created things—created things ask "where do you come from"; God does not answer this question, because this question is meaningless to God. God says "I Am That I Am"—not "where do I come from," but "I Am." Origin is a category of time and space; God is not within time and space. God does not need an origin, because origin is the concept of "beginning," and God has no beginning.

> God is man, the Void is woman. Without man, where does woman come from? Man comes from the dust, not born of woman. Woman is merely the vessel of man's operation, and man is woman's origin. God certainly does not need to obey a woman or anyone.
