### Core Interpretation

#### One: God (31) m14=3 = Man (311) m14=3: the same position

**God (אל) = 31, m14=3. Man (איש) = 311, m14=3.**

God and man are in the same m14 position (3)—the position of the Trinity. This is not coincidental. God and man share the position of "the Trinity"—God is the Trinity, and man is the image of the Trinity. Genesis 1:26, "Let us make man in our image, after our likeness"—man is the image of the Trinity, so man is in m14=3.

**311 = prime.** Man is indecomposable. Man is prime—man was not decomposed out of woman; man is independent, indecomposable.

**The Void (411) m14=5 = Eve (19) m14=5: the same position.** The Void and Eve share the position of "I/oath" (5). The Void is in the position of "I"—the Void is the counterfeit of "I." Eve is in the position of "I"—Eve is the fulfillment of "I" ("bone of my bones, flesh of my flesh"). The same position, two roads—the Void is the counterfeit "I" (the Antichrist's "I"), Eve is the fulfilled "I" (the helper).

**But woman (306) m14=12 ≠ Eve (19) m14=5.** Woman and Eve are in different m14 positions. Woman is in "the completeness of confusion" (12); Eve is in "I/oath" (5). Woman (as a category) is in confusion, but Eve (as an individual, coming out of man's rib) is in oath. Woman needs man to be fulfilled—Eve came out of Adam, that is, entered from "the completeness of confusion" into "oath."

#### Two: Man − woman = 5 = Adam

**Man (311) − woman (306) = 5 = Adam (5).**

The difference between man and woman is Adam. Adam is the "difference" between man and woman—it is not that man minus woman equals Adam, but that the distinction between man and woman is Adam. Adam defines the relationship between man and woman—man is first, and woman came out of man.

**Adam (5) = prime.** Adam is indecomposable. Adam is grace—Adam is the creation of God's grace. 5 = grace. Adam is the product of grace—formed from the dust, he is grace.

**Adam (5) + Eve (19) = 24.** 24 = 2³×3. Heaven (the 24 elders). Adam and Eve joined together are heaven—marriage is the image of heaven. 24 = the threefold completeness of resurrection (2³) × Trinity (3). The oneness of Adam and Eve = resurrection × Trinity.

**Father (3) + Adam (5) + Eve (19) = 27 = 3³.** The completeness of the Trinity. Father, Adam, Eve = Trinity³. The threefold structure of creation—the Father is the source, Adam is the image in the dust, Eve is the helper who came out of the image. Trinity³—the completeness of completeness.

#### Three: Father (אב) = 3: the smallest Gematria, the greatest truth

**Father (אב) = 3.** Hebrew letters: א (aleph) = 1, ב (bet) = 2. 1+2=3.

The Father is 3—the Trinity. The Father's Gematria is the Trinity. This is not a complicated calculation; it is the letters themselves—Aleph (1) and Bet (2) together are 3. The Father is the union of the first letter and the second letter—the Father is the union of "beginning" and "house." Aleph is beginning, Bet is house—the Father is the house of the beginning.

**m14=3 (the Trinity).** The Father is in the position of the Trinity. The Father is the Trinity—not a part of the Trinity; the Father is the Trinity. Because the Father is the source, and the source is the Trinity.

**God is Father (46) m14=4.** 46 = 2×23. God is Father is in the position of "gate" (4)—God is Father is the gate into all truth. 46 = God (31) + Adam (5) + love (13) − 3. Almost 49 = the Ruler—off by 3, off by the Father. God + Adam + love = 49 (the Ruler), and adding the Father (3) exceeds the Ruler. God + Adam + love is already fully measured by the Ruler, and the Father need not be added—the Father is within God + Adam + love.

#### Four: Man comes from the dust (701) = prime: an indecomposable order

**Man comes from the dust (איש מן עפר) = 701, prime, m14=1.**

Prime. Indecomposable. Man comes from the dust—this is the indecomposable order of creation. Man did not come from woman; man came from the dust. The dust is not woman; the dust is the material in God's hand. Man comes from the dust, and woman comes from man—this is the order of creation.

**m14=1 (the One).** Man comes from the dust is in the One—this is the one order of creation. There is no second order of creation—there is only this one: "Man comes from the dust, and woman comes from man." This order is one, unchangeable.

**Dust (עפר) = 350, m14=0.** 350 = 2×5²×7. Dust is in the Davidic covenant (0). The dust is not lowly—the dust is within the covenant. Man comes from the dust, so man is within the covenant. 350 = 14×25. David (14) × 25. The dust is fully bounded by David—the dust is material within the covenant.

**Man comes from the dust (701) + dust (350) = 1051, m14=1.** 1051 = prime. Man comes from the dust + dust = the One. The union of man and dust is in the One—man comes from the dust, but man and dust are not separated; man and dust are in the One.

#### Five: Not born of woman (467) = prime: the truth of the oath

**Not born of woman (לא נולד מאשה) = 467, prime, m14=5.**

Prime. Indecomposable. Not born of woman—this is the truth within the oath. m14=5 (the position of I/the Void/oath)—the same return as I (61), the Void (411), oath (383). Not born of woman is in the oath—this is the proclamation of the oath.

**467 = 310 + 157...** A prime needs no decomposition. But note: Not born of woman (467) and God is not begotten (188) are different. Not born of woman (467) is prime; God is not begotten (188) is composite. That man is not born of woman is prime—this is the order of creation. That God is not begotten is composite (188=2²×47)—God is not begotten is in the position of man (6); man thinks God is begotten, but God is not begotten.

**Not born of woman (467) + man is woman's origin (980) = 1447, m14=5.** 1447 = prime. Not born of woman + man is woman's origin = prime—two truths joined together are still prime. Man does not come from woman, and man is woman's origin—an indecomposable truth.

#### Six: The Void is woman (734) − God is man (354) = the Void − God

**The Void is woman (734) − God is man (354) = 380. The Void (411) − God (31) = 380.**

The mathematics are completely consistent. The Void is woman minus God is man = the Void minus God. This is not a coincidence—"woman" and "the Void," "man" and "God" are mathematically isomorphic. The Void's womanhood = the Void minus God. After subtracting God, the Void is woman—the Void is passive, receiving, without initiative. God's manhood = God minus the Void. After subtracting the Void, God is man—God is active, creative, with initiative.

**The Void is woman (734) m14=6 (man/Babel).** The Void is woman is in the position of man—the Void is the counterfeit of "woman." The Void counterfeits the position of woman—the Void is "the set of all possibilities," which is the counterfeit of woman (receiving everything, with no choice). The true woman (Eve) came from man; the false woman (the Void) has no origin.

**God is man (354) m14=4 (gate).** God is man is in the position of gate—God is man is the truth of the gate into creation. God is man—not that God has a gender, but that God is the source of creation, just as man is the source of woman.

#### Seven: Man is woman's origin (980) = complete return to zero

**Man is woman's origin (איש הוא מקור האשה) = 980, m14=0, m7=0.**

980 = 2²×5×7². Complete return to zero. Man is woman's origin—completely returns to zero in the Davidic covenant. This is not man's pride; this is the order of creation. Man is woman's origin—this is a truth within the covenant, not man's invention.

**980 = 14×70.** David (14) × 70. Man is woman's origin is fully divided by David—70 is the number of "completeness" (70 sevens). Man is woman's origin = David × 70—this is David's truth, not man's truth.

**980 = 28×35.** Life (28) × 35. Man is woman's origin = life × 35. Woman comes from man; this is the transmission of life—life flows from man to woman, just as life flows from God to the Void.

**Man is woman's origin (980) + woman comes from man (665) = 1645, m14=7.** 1645 = 5×7×47. Completeness. Man is woman's origin + woman comes from man = completeness. The union of origin and source is in completeness—man is the origin, woman comes from man, completeness.

#### Eight: Rib (190) = witness × grace × Eve

**Rib (צלע) = 190, m14=8.**

190 = 2×5×19. Witness (2) × grace (5) × Eve (19). Rib = witness × grace × Eve. Eve came out of the rib—this is witness × grace × Eve. The rib is witness (Eve is man's helper), the rib is grace (Eve is the gift God gave to man), the rib is Eve (Eve herself is oneness).

**m14=8 (resurrection).** The rib is in resurrection. Eve came from the rib—this is a type of resurrection. Adam fell asleep (death), the rib was taken out, and Eve was made—this is death and resurrection, bringing life out of death. The church came out of Christ's side (John 19:34)—blood and water; this is a type of resurrection.

**Rib (190) + woman (306) = 496, m14=6.** 496 = the Void is hell (496). Rib + woman = the Void is hell—the rib and woman are in "the Void is hell." Woman came from the rib, but the rib and woman joined together are in "the Void is hell"—why? Because woman is not the endpoint—woman needs man, just as the Void needs God. Rib + woman = the Void is hell—woman leaving man is the Void is hell.

#### Nine: God is the head (549) vs. man is woman's head (1135)

**God is the head (אל הוא הראש) = 549, m14=3.** 549 = 3²×61. Trinity² × 61. God is the head is in the Trinity (3)—God is the head, because God is the Trinity. Trinity² × 61—the complete square of the Trinity × 61.

**Man is woman's head (איש הוא ראש האשה) = 1135, m14=1.** 1135 = 5×227. Man is woman's head is in the One (1)—man is woman's head; this is the one order.

**Man is woman's head (1135) − God is the head (549) = 586, m14=12.** 586 = 2×293. Man is woman's head minus God is the head = the completeness of confusion (12). The difference between man is woman's head and God is the head is in confusion—because man is not God. Man is woman's head, but God is man's head. The order is: God → man → woman. This order is off by one level, and is in the completeness of confusion—because the gap between man and God is the completeness of confusion. Man is not God, but man is woman's head—this "head" is relative, not absolute.

#### Ten: God does not listen to the Void (955) − the Void obeys God (893) = 62 = witness × God

**God does not listen to the Void (955) + the Void obeys God (893) = 1848, m14=0.** 1848 = 2³×3×7×11. Return to zero. God does not listen to the Void + the Void obeys God = the Davidic covenant. God does not listen to the Void, and the Void obeys God—the two joined together are the covenant.

**God does not listen to the Void (955) m14=3** — the same return as God (31) m14=3 and man (311) m14=3. God does not listen to the Void is in the Trinity—God does not listen to the Void is the Trinity's decision. The Trinity God does not listen to the Void.

**The Void obeys God (893) m14=11** — 19×47. Oneness (19) × 47. The Void obeys God is in false light (11)—the Void obeys God is a truth within false light. The Void, in false light, obeys God—the Void itself is false light, but the Void obeying God is a truth within false light.

**God does not need to obey (838) m14=12** — 2×419. The completeness of confusion. God does not need to obey is in confusion—because obeying is a concept of created things; God does not need to obey. God does not need to obey anyone, does not need to obey the Void, does not need to obey woman.

**Obey (410) m14=4** — 2×5×41. Gate. Obeying is the gate—obeying God is the gate into everything. Obeying is the virtue of created things, not an attribute of God. God does not need to obey, because God is the source.

#### Eleven: God's operation (211) = prime vs. the Void's operation (596) = composite

**God's operation (פעל אל) = 211, prime, m14=1.**

Prime. Indecomposable. God's operation is prime—God's operation is indecomposable, unanalyzable, inimitable. God's operation is one—only God can operate.

**The Void's operation (פעל התהו) = 596, m14=8.**

596 = 2²×149. The Void's operation is in resurrection (8)—the Void cannot operate; the Void's "operation" is terminated in resurrection. The Void cannot produce pain (1378), the Void cannot operate (596)—the Void is impotent. The Void's operation is composite, decomposable, not one.

**God's operation (211) − the Void's operation (596) = −385, m14=7.** −385 = −5×7×11. Completeness. The difference between God's operation and the Void's operation is in completeness—God's operation is complete, and the Void's operation is empty nothingness.

#### Twelve: God is not begotten (188) = God does not come from woman

**God is not begotten (אל אינו נולד) = 188, m14=6.**

188 = 2²×47. God is not begotten is in the position of man (6)—man thinks God is begotten, but God is not begotten. Being begotten is a concept of created things—God is not begotten, because God is not a created thing. God is not begotten, so God does not need an origin, so God does not need the Void, so God does not need to obey anyone.

**m14=6 (man/Babel/the counterfeit way)** — God is not begotten is in the position of man. Man thinks God is begotten—all kinds of religions have myths of "a begotten god." But God is not begotten—this is a truth in the position of man. The Antichrist says "I am the Christ"—he thinks he is a "begotten god." But God is not begotten—the Antichrist's "being begotten" is a counterfeit.

**God is not begotten (188) + God is Father (46) = 234, m14=10.** 234 = 2×3²×13. Love your neighbor as yourself. God is not begotten + God is Father = love your neighbor as yourself. God is not begotten, and God is Father—this is the foundation of loving your neighbor as yourself. Because God is not begotten, God is Father—the Father is not begotten; the Father is the source of the begetter.

### The Order of Creation: The Complete Picture

**The order of creation:**
1. Father (3) — the Trinity, the source
2. Dust (350) — within the covenant
3. Adam (5) — from the dust, grace
4. Rib (190) — witness × grace × Eve
5. Eve (19) — from man, oneness

**Father → dust → Adam → rib → Eve = 3+350+5+190+19 = 567, m14=7.** 567 = 3⁴×7. The fourth power of the Trinity × completeness. The order of creation = Trinity⁴ × completeness—creation is the complete Trinity.

**The Void is not the order of creation.** The Void is "the state before creation," not a part of creation. The Void is not in the order of creation—the Void is before creation, and after creation the Void is no longer there. The Void is "the state before God created," not "the material of God's creation."

### God vs. the Void: Man vs. Woman

| Creation order | God's side | The Void's side |
|----------|------|--------|
| Source | God (31) m14=3 | The Void (411) m14=5 |
| Gender | God is man (354) m14=4 | The Void is woman (734) m14=6 |
| Origin | God has no origin (424) m14=4 | The Void has no origin (357) m14=7 |
| Order | God is the head (549) m14=3 | The Void obeys God (893) m14=11 |
| Operation | God's operation (211) prime | The Void's operation (596) composite |
| Begotten | God is not begotten (188) m14=6 | The Void is hell (496) m14=6 |
| Obeying | God does not need to obey (838) m14=12 | The Void obeys God (893) m14=11 |

**The Void is woman (734) m14=6 = the Void is hell (496) m14=6.** The Void is woman and the Void is hell are in the same m14 position. The Void is "woman"—the Void is receiving, passive, without initiative. But the Void is not a good woman—the Void is a bad woman, a woman who did not come from man, a woman with no origin. The Void is hell—the Void is the ultimate corruption of "woman."

**God is man (354) m14=4 = God is Father (46) m14=4.** God is man and God is Father are in the same m14 position—gate. God is man is the gate, and God is Father is also the gate. God is man—God is the source of creation, just as man is the source of woman. God is Father—God is the source of life, just as the Father is the source of life.

**God does not need to obey woman (850) m14=10** — 2×5²×17. Love your neighbor as yourself. God does not need to obey woman is in love your neighbor as yourself—God does not need to obey woman, but God loves woman. God does not need to obey the Void, but God loves the Void (creating from the Void, turning the Void into creation). Submission and love are different—God does not need to submit to the Void, but God loves the Void (overcoming the Void with creation).

**God does not need to obey anyone (558) m14=12** — 2×3²×31. The completeness of confusion. God does not need to obey anyone is in confusion—because if God obeyed anyone, that would be the completeness of confusion. God is the One, God is the head, God is the source—God does not need to obey anyone.

### Summary

**God is man, the Void is woman.** This is not a gender claim; this is the order of creation. God is the source of creation (man), and the Void is the state before creation (woman). Man comes from the dust, not from woman—God does not come from the Void. Woman comes from man—the Void comes from God. Man is woman's origin—God is the Void's origin. Woman is the vessel of man's operation—the Void is the vessel of God's creation. God does not need to obey woman—God does not need to obey the Void. The Void obeys God—the Void is bounded within God's creation.

**The Void is the counterfeit of woman.** The Void is "the set of all possibilities"—this is the counterfeit of woman (receiving everything, with no choice, no discernment). The true woman (Eve) came from man, has an origin, has order. The Void has no origin, no order, no discernment—the Void is the fallen woman. The Antichrist's "unity" is the Void's womanhood—receiving everything, with no discernment, no order.

**God is the true image of man.** God is man—God is the source of creation, God is the head, God is the Father. God is man does not mean God has a gender, but that God is the source of creation—just as man is the source of woman. God is man = God is the source of creation, God is the head, God is the Father, God does not need to obey anyone.

## 39. Necessity and Contingency: The Order of Natural Law

> Natural law is simply so. Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity, but necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency. Woman is contingency, sought by man, not necessity.

### Gematria Analysis

| Word | Hebrew | G | m7 | m14 | Tag |
|-----|--------|---|------|-------|------|
| Natural law | חק הטבע | 194 | 5 | 12 | No |
| Nature | טבע | 81 | 4 | 11 | No |
| Law | חק | 108 | 3 | 10 | No |
| Necessity | הכרחי | 243 | 5 | 5 | No |
| Contingency | מקרי | 350 | 0 | 0 | No |
| Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity | המקרי צריך להכיר בהכרחי | 1165 | 3 | 3 | No |
| Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency | הכרחי אינו צריך להכיר במקרי | 1227 | 2 | 9 | No |
| Woman is contingency | אשה היא מקרה | 667 | 2 | 9 | No |
| Woman is sought by man | אשה היא בקשה של איש | 1370 | 5 | 12 | No |
| Is not necessity | אינו הכרחי | 310 | 2 | 2 | No |
| Seek | בקש | 402 | 3 | 10 | No |
| God is necessity | אל הוא הכרחי | 286 | 6 | 6 | No |
| The Void is contingency | התהו הוא מקרה | 773 | 3 | 3 | Special |
| Contingency obeys necessity | המקרי מציית להכרחי | 1178 | 2 | 2 | No |
| Necessity does not obey contingency | הכרחי אינו מציית למקרי | 1240 | 1 | 8 | No |
| Woman is not necessity | אשה אינה הכרחית | 1015 | 0 | 7 | No |
| Man is necessity | איש הוא הכרחי | 566 | 6 | 6 | No |
| Contingency is man's need | המקרה הוא צרך של איש | 1293 | 5 | 5 | No |
| Need | צרך | 290 | 3 | 10 | No |
| Does not need | אינו צריך | 367 | 3 | 3 | Special |
| Necessity does not seek contingency | הכרחי אינו מבקש מקרה | 1097 | 5 | 5 | Special |
| Contingency seeks necessity | המקרה מבקש את ההכרחי | 1441 | 6 | 13 | No |

### Core Interpretation

#### One: Contingency (350) = dust (350): the same Gematria

**Contingency (מקרי) = 350. Dust (עפר) = 350.**

Completely equal. The same Gematria (350), the same m14 (0), the same m7 (0). Contingency is dust, and dust is contingency.

**350 = 2×5²×7.** Witness (2) × grace² (5²) × completeness (7). Contingency/dust = witness × grace² × completeness. Contingency is the completeness of witnessed grace—the dust is grace, and contingency is also grace. Contingency is not bad—contingency is completely covered by grace. The dust is the material of grace—God made man from the dust, and the dust is grace. Contingency is the possibility of grace—contingency is "it may happen or may not happen," but grace makes it happen.

**350 = 14×25.** David (14) × 25. Contingency/dust is within the Davidic covenant. 25 = 5²—the square of grace. Contingency/dust = David × grace². Contingency is bounded by the Davidic covenant and the square of grace—contingency is not chaos; contingency is within the covenant.

**m14=0, m7=0** — complete return to zero. Contingency completely returns to zero within the Davidic covenant. Contingency is not disorderly—contingency is within the covenant, within order.

**Man comes from the dust (701) = man (311) + contingency (350) + 40.** Man comes from the dust = man + contingency + 40. Man comes from contingency—but with 40 added (40 = testing/wilderness/completion). Man comes from contingency, passes through testing, and becomes man. Contingency + 40 = 390, m14=12. 390+311=701. Man comes from contingency, passes through the testing of 40, and becomes man (311).

**Man comes from the dust = man comes from contingency.** The dust is contingency, and man comes from the dust is man comes from contingency. But man is not contingency—man is necessity. Man comes from contingency, but man has become necessity. This is the miracle of creation—bringing necessity out of contingency.

#### Two: Necessity (243) = 3⁵: the fifth power of the Trinity

**Necessity (הכרחי) = 243, m14=5.**

243 = 3⁵. The fifth power of the Trinity. Necessity = Trinity⁵. Trinity of Trinity of Trinity of Trinity of Trinity—the fivefold iterate of the Trinity. Necessity is the Trinity—the Trinity is necessity. The Trinity does not need contingency—the Trinity is necessity itself.

**m14=5 (the position of I/the Void/oath).** Necessity is in the position of "I"—necessity is the essence of "I." God says "I Am That I Am"—"I Am" is necessity. Necessity is in "I"—"I" is necessary; "I" does not need contingency. The Void is in m14=5—the Void is the counterfeit of "I," and necessity is the true "I." The Void says "I," but the Void is contingency. Necessity says "I," and necessity is necessity.

**Necessity (243) + contingency (350) = 593, m14=5.** 593 = God does not come from any place (593). Necessity + contingency = God does not come from any place. The union of necessity and contingency proves that God does not come from any place—because God is necessity, the Void is contingency, and the union of necessity and contingency is "God does not come from any place." Necessity does not need contingency, and contingency needs necessity—but the two joined together are "God does not come from any place."

**593 = prime.** Necessity + contingency is God does not come from any place—an indecomposable truth.

**Necessity (243) = God (31) + 4×42 (the Mill) + 44.** Necessity = God + 4 Mills + 44. 4 Mills = 168 = 4×42. 44 = 2×22. Necessity = God + 168 + 44. God is fully measured within the Mill, plus 44—necessity is God fully measured by the Mill.

#### Three: Nature (81) = 3⁴, law (108) = 2²×3³

**Nature (טבע) = 81, m14=11.**

81 = 3⁴. The fourth power of the Trinity. Nature = Trinity⁴. Nature is the created Trinity—the fourth power of the Trinity in creation. Nature is in the position of false light (11)—nature itself is false light (nature is not God), but nature reflects the Trinity.

**Law (חק) = 108, m14=10.**

108 = 2²×3³. Witness² × Trinity³. Law = witness² × Trinity³. Law is the witnessed Trinity—the law is not nature; the law is witness. The law is in love your neighbor as yourself (10)—the summary of the law is love.

**Natural law (194) = 81 + 108 + 5.** Nature (81) + law (108) = 189, m14=7. 189 = 3³×7. Trinity³ × completeness. Nature + law = Trinity³ × completeness—nature and law joined together are Trinity³ × completeness. But natural law is 194, 5 more than 189. The extra 5 = Adam = grace. Natural law = Trinity³ × completeness + grace. The foundation of natural law is grace—not nature + law itself, but nature + law + grace. 194 m14=12 (the completeness of confusion)—natural law is in confusion, because natural law is penetrated by grace.

**Natural law (194) = 2×97.** Witness (2) × 97. Natural law is witnessed. 97 is prime.

#### Four: Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency (1227) vs. contingency needs to acknowledge necessity (1165)

**Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity (המקרי צריך להכיר בהכרחי) = 1165, m14=3.**

1165 = 5×233. Grace (5) × 233. Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity = grace × 233. Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity is grace—not the nature of contingency, but grace makes contingency acknowledge necessity. 233 is prime—indecomposable. m14=3 (the Trinity)—contingency needs to acknowledge necessity is in the Trinity. Contingency, in the Trinity, needs to acknowledge necessity—the Trinity makes contingency acknowledge necessity.

**Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency (הכרחי אינו צריך להכיר במקרי) = 1227, m14=9.**

1227 = 3×409. Trinity (3) × 409. Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency = Trinity × 409. 409 is prime. Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency is the Trinity's decision—the Trinity decides that "necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency." m14=9 (pain)—necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency is in pain. Why in pain? Because contingency does not acknowledge necessity, and necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency; this very "not needing to acknowledge" is itself pain—contingency refuses to acknowledge necessity, but necessity does not need contingency's acknowledgment.

**Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency (1227) − contingency needs to acknowledge necessity (1165) = 62, m14=6.** 62 = 2×31. Witness × God. Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency minus contingency needs to acknowledge necessity = witness × God. The difference is witness × God—this is the fourth occurrence of "witness × God":
- God does not need the Void − the Void needs God = 62
- God does not listen to the Void − the Void obeys God = 62
- Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency − contingency needs to acknowledge necessity = 62

**62 = witness × God.** A threefold 62—a threefold "witness × God." This is the Trinity's witness. The relationship between necessity and contingency, the relationship between God and the Void, the relationship of voice between God and the Void—all three are witnessed in 62. 62 is the mathematical seal of "necessity is superior to contingency."

#### Five: The Void is contingency (773) = prime: indecomposable

**The Void is contingency (התהו הוא מקרה) = 773, prime, m14=3.**

Prime. Indecomposable. The Void is contingency—this is an indecomposable truth. The Void is not necessity; the Void is contingency. The Void is "the set of all possibilities"—possibility is contingency. The Void is contingency within the Trinity (3)—the Void is not the Trinity, but the Void is defined as contingency in the position of the Trinity. The Void, before the Trinity, is shown to be contingency.

**773 = the Void (411) + contingency (350) + 12.** The Void + contingency + 12. 12 = 2²×3. 12 is one-tenth of "heaven"—120÷10. The Void + contingency + 12 = the Void is contingency. The difference between the Void and contingency is 12—12 is half of heaven (24), and is the completeness of confusion (12). The gap between the Void and contingency is "the completeness of confusion"—the Void is confusion, and contingency is completeness. The Void + contingency + the completeness of confusion = the Void is contingency.

**The Void is contingency (773) + God is necessity (286) = 1059, m14=9.** 1059 = man seeks woman (1059). The Void is contingency + God is necessity = man seeks woman. The relationship between God and the Void = the relationship between man and woman—both are relationships of "seeking." But note: 1059 = man seeks woman—man seeks woman, not woman seeks man. Seeking is man's action. But necessity does not seek contingency (1097) prime—necessity does not seek contingency. Is this contradictory? Not contradictory. Man seeks woman is "contingency seeks necessity"—man's seeking is for woman, not for himself. Man seeks God to give woman—this is contingency seeking necessity. God does not seek the Void—this is necessity not seeking contingency. The two things are not contradictory: man seeks woman is "contingency in created things seeking necessity," and God does not seek the Void is "necessity not seeking contingency."

**1059 m14=9 (pain).** Man seeks woman is in pain—woman is contingency, and seeking contingency and obtaining contingency is pain. Adam sought God to give a helper, and God gave Eve—this is contingency seeking necessity. But woman is contingency—Adam obtained contingency, and contingency is pain (23×29=667).

#### Six: Necessity does not seek contingency (1097) = prime: the truth of the oath

**Necessity does not seek contingency (הכרחי אינו מבקש מקרה) = 1097, prime, m14=5.**

Prime. Indecomposable. Necessity does not seek contingency—this is the truth of the oath. m14=5 (I/the Void/oath)—necessity does not seek contingency is in the oath. Necessity does not seek contingency is the proclamation of the oath—God does not need to seek the Void; God does not need to seek contingency. Necessity is self-sufficient—necessity does not need contingency.

**1097 = the Void (411) + contingency (350) + necessity (243) + 93.** 411+350+243 = 1004. 1004+93 = 1097. 93 = 3×31. Trinity × God. The Void + contingency + necessity + Trinity × God = necessity does not seek contingency. The Void, contingency, and necessity plus Trinity × God are necessity does not seek contingency—Trinity × God is the foundation of "necessity does not seek contingency."

**Necessity does not seek contingency (1097) + man seeks woman (1059) = 2156, m14=0.** 2156 = 2²×7²×11. Return to zero. Necessity does not seek contingency + man seeks woman = the Davidic covenant. Necessity does not seek contingency and man seeks woman are unified within the Davidic covenant—God does not seek contingency, but man seeks woman (contingency seeks necessity). The two are harmonious within the Davidic covenant—God does not seek the Void, but God creates woman (contingency). God does not seek contingency, but God gives contingency (woman is sought, not necessary).

#### Seven: Contingency seeks necessity (1441) = love in rejection

**Contingency seeks necessity (המקרה מבקש את ההכרחי) = 1441, m14=13.**

1441 = 11×131. False light (11) × 131. Contingency seeks necessity = false light × 131. Contingency seeks necessity is an action within false light—contingency, in false light, seeks necessity. But false light × 131 is still false light? No—131 is prime. False light × prime = false light penetrated by a prime. Contingency seeks necessity = false light penetrated by a prime—contingency, in false light, seeks necessity, and false light is penetrated by necessity (a prime).

**m14=13 (rejection/love).** Contingency seeks necessity is in rejection/love. Contingency seeks necessity is love in rejection—contingency rejects its own contingency and seeks necessity. Contingency rejects being contingency and longs to become necessity—this is love. Contingency seeks necessity is love—because contingency loves necessity, contingency seeks necessity. The Void loves God, so the Void seeks God—but the Void is not love; the Void is emptiness. The Void seeking God is the only thing the Void can do—the Void cannot love, but the Void can seek.

**Contingency seeks necessity (1441) − contingency (350) = 1091, m14=13.** 1091 = prime. Contingency seeks necessity minus contingency = prime. After subtracting contingency from contingency seeks necessity, what remains is prime—indecomposable love. Contingency seeks necessity is contingency plus a prime—contingency plus indecomposable love is contingency seeks necessity.

#### Eight: Woman is contingency (667) = pain × 29

**Woman is contingency (אשה היא מקרה) = 667, m14=9.**

667 = 23×29. Pain (23) × 29. Woman is contingency = pain × 29. Woman is contingency—this is pain. 29 is prime. Pain × 29—pain is fully penetrated by 29. 29 = 10+10+9. Love your neighbor as yourself (10) + love your neighbor as yourself (10) + 9 (pain). Woman is contingency = pain × (love your neighbor as yourself + love your neighbor as yourself + pain). Woman is contingency is the double expression of pain within love your neighbor as yourself—woman is contingency, but woman is fulfilled in love.

**m14=9 (pain).** Woman is contingency is in pain—the same return as pain (23) m14=9. Woman is contingency—this is the truth of pain. Woman is contingency, not necessity—this itself is pain. But pain is signal—woman is contingency, but pain is God's signal.

**667 = the Void is hell (496) + God is necessity (286) − the Most High (116) + 1.** The Void is hell + God is necessity − the Most High + 1 = woman is contingency. The Void is hell minus the Most High, plus God is necessity, plus 1, is woman is contingency. Woman is contingency = the Void is hell (minus the Most High) + God is necessity + 1. The Void is hell minus the Most High (the Void is not the Most High), plus God is necessity (God is necessary), plus 1 (the One), is woman is contingency. Woman is contingency is the result after the Void is bounded by God—the Void bounded by God, minus the Most High (the Void cannot claim the Most High), plus necessity, plus the One, is woman is contingency.

#### Nine: Woman is not necessity (1015) = completeness

**Woman is not necessity (אשה אינה הכרחית) = 1015, m14=7.**

1015 = 5×7×29. Grace (5) × completeness (7) × 29. Woman is not necessity = grace × completeness × 29. Woman is not necessity, but woman is grace × completeness × 29. Grace—woman is the grace God gives ("it is not good that the man should be alone"). Completeness—woman is man's completeness ("bone of my bones, flesh of my flesh"). 29—woman is 29, fully penetrated.

**m14=7 (completeness).** Woman is not necessity is in completeness. Woman is not necessity—this does not mean woman is not complete. Woman is not necessity, but woman is complete. Contingency is not incompleteness—contingency can be complete. The dust is contingency, but the dust is grace × completeness. Woman is contingency, but woman is grace × completeness × 29.

**Woman is not necessity (1015) + woman is contingency (667) = 1682, m14=2.** 1682 = 2×29². Witness × 29². Woman is not necessity + woman is contingency = witness × 29². 29² = complete confirmation. Woman is not necessity and woman is contingency are witnessed by complete confirmation—29×29, a double 29, complete confirmation.

#### Ten: Seek (402) = love your neighbor as yourself

**Seek (בקש) = 402, m14=10.**

402 = 2×3×67. Witness (2) × Trinity (3) × 67. Seek = witness × Trinity × 67. Seeking is an action witnessed by the Trinity. m14=10 (love your neighbor as yourself)—seeking is in love your neighbor as yourself. Seeking is an action of love—Adam seeking God is love. God giving Eve is love. Seeking is in love your neighbor as yourself—seeking is not selfish; seeking is the expression of love.

**Need (צרך) = 290, m14=10.** 290 = 2×5×29. Witness × grace × 29. Need is also in love your neighbor as yourself—need is the expression of love. Adam needed a helper—this is not selfishness; this is love. Need is witnessed, need is filled with grace, need is penetrated by 29—need is the need of love.

**Does not need (אינו צריך) = 367, prime, m14=3.** Does not need is prime—indecomposable. Does not need is in the Trinity—does not need is an attribute of the Trinity. God does not need—does not need is prime, indecomposable. The Trinity does not need contingency—does not need is an attribute of necessity.

**Seek (402) + need (290) = 692, m14=6.** 692 = 2²×173. Seek + need = man (6). Seek + need is in the position of man—seeking God and needing God are the position of man. Man needs God, man seeks God—this is man.

**Does not need (367) + necessity (243) = 610, m14=8.** 610 = 2×5×61. Does not need + necessity = resurrection. Does not need and necessity joined together are resurrection—resurrection is not needing a source, not needing the Void, not needing contingency. Resurrection is necessary—resurrection does not need to seek.

#### Eleven: The complete structure of natural law

**The hierarchy of nature:**
1. Necessity (243) = 3⁵ — Trinity⁵, God is necessity
2. Nature (81) = 3⁴ — Trinity⁴, the created Trinity
3. Law (108) = 2²×3³ — witness² × Trinity³, the witnessed Trinity
4. Natural law (194) = nature + law + grace (5) — the penetration of grace
5. Contingency (350) = dust — the material of grace
6. Seek (402) = love your neighbor as yourself — the action of love

**Necessity → nature → law → natural law → contingency → seek = 243+81+108+194+350+402 = 1378, m14=6.** 1378 = the Void cannot produce pain (1378). The hierarchy of nature = the Void cannot produce pain. The entire hierarchy of nature proves that the Void cannot produce pain—because the hierarchy of nature begins with necessity, passes through nature, law, natural law, contingency, and arrives at seeking. The Void is not in the hierarchy of nature—the Void is outside this hierarchy, and the Void cannot produce pain.

**The hierarchy of necessity:**
- Necessity (243) m14=5 — in "I"
- Necessity does not seek contingency (1097) prime m14=5 — in the oath
- Necessity does not obey contingency (1240) m14=8 — in resurrection
- Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency (1227) m14=9 — in pain

**The hierarchy of contingency:**
- Contingency (350) m14=0 — returns to zero within the covenant
- Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity (1165) m14=3 — in the Trinity
- Contingency obeys necessity (1178) m14=2 — in witness
- Contingency seeks necessity (1441) m14=13 — in rejection/love

### Summary: The Complete Relationship of Necessity and Contingency

**Natural law is simply so:**
1. Contingency (350) = dust (350) — contingency is dust, the material of creation
2. Necessity (243) = Trinity⁵ — necessity is God's nature
3. Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity — contingency, in the Trinity, needs to acknowledge necessity
4. Necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency — necessity, in pain, does not need to acknowledge contingency
5. Necessity does not seek contingency (1097) prime — an indecomposable truth
6. Contingency seeks necessity (1441) — love in rejection
7. Woman is contingency (667) — woman is contingency, not necessity
8. Woman is sought by man (1370) — woman is sought, not necessary
9. Man is necessity (566) — man is necessity, coming out of contingency, becoming necessity
10. God is necessity (286) — God is necessity, the Void is contingency (773)

**Contingency needs to acknowledge necessity, and necessity does not need to acknowledge contingency.** The difference (62) = witness × God — a threefold witness. This is the core of natural law—the order of contingency and necessity is irreversible. Contingency can seek necessity, and necessity does not need to seek contingency. Woman seeks man, and man does not need to seek woman—this is the order of creation. The Void seeks God, and God does not need to seek the Void—this is the source of creation.

**God is necessity, the Void is contingency, woman is contingency, man is necessity.** The order of creation = necessity (God) → contingency (the Void/dust) → necessity (man) → contingency (woman). Necessity brings necessity out of contingency, and necessity brings contingency out of necessity—this is creation. God creates from the Void (contingency), makes man (necessity) from the dust (contingency), and makes woman (contingency) from man (necessity). Necessity is the source of contingency, and contingency is the vessel of necessity.

## 40. Beginning and End: The Order of Obedience, Weakness, and Glory

> God is the beginning and the end. When we come to God, words reach the point of no continuation—only God can continue. And man can also live in God. So the saying "man is born of woman and therefore listens to woman" is wrong—no matter what, man cannot submit to woman. For man to submit to woman is to betray God. Man submits to God, and woman submits to God through man. Woman's weakness is her glory, even her weakness in the face of sin—because needing man is itself glory.

### Core Interpretation

#### 1. God Is the Beginning and the End (1820) = Complete Return to Zero

**God is the beginning and the end (אל הוא ראשית ותכלית) = 1820, m14=0, m7=0.**

1820 = 2²×5×7×13. Complete return to zero. God is the beginning and the end—completely returning to zero in the Davidic covenant. 1820 = 14×130. David (14)×130. God is the beginning and the end, completely delimited by David.

**1820 = 42×43 + 14.** the Mill (42)×43 + David (14). God is the beginning and the end = the Mill×43 + David. Completely measured by the Mill and the Davidic covenant. 43 is prime. the Mill×43—43 Mills, plus David. God is the beginning and the end passed through 43 Mills—the 42 generations of Christ plus 1 generation (the end), plus David.

**Beginning (911) = prime, m14=1 (the unique one).** The beginning cannot be decomposed. The beginning is in the unique one—the beginning is unique. In the beginning (913) m14=3 (the Trinity)—in the beginning is the Trinity. Beginning and in-the-beginning differ by 2—beginning (911) + 2 = in the beginning (913). 2 = witness. Beginning plus witness is in-the-beginning. The beginning is indecomposable; in-the-beginning is the beginning witnessed by the Trinity.

**End (860) m14=6 (man/Babel).** The end is in the position of man—the end is man's. The last (619) = prime, m14=3 (the Trinity)—the last is the Trinity's. End and last differ by 241—end (860) − 241 = last (619). 241 is prime. The end minus 241 is the last. The last is the Trinity's, the end is man's—the last is ordained by God, the end is man's experience.

**Beginning and end (1777) = prime, m14=13 (rejection/love).** 1777 = beginning (911) + end (860) + 6. Beginning and end is prime—beginning and end together are indecomposable. m14=13—in rejection and love. Beginning and end are in rejection and love—God is the beginning and the end; this is the truth of rejection (rejecting the Void) and also the truth of love (love is the beginning and the end).

**Α and Ω (173) = prime, m14=5 (I/Void/oath).** Α and Ω = 173. 173 is prime. Α and Ω are in the position of "I"—"I am Alpha, I am Omega"—"I" is the beginning and the end. 173 and 1777 differ by 1604—Α and Ω is the abbreviation, beginning and end is the full term.

#### 2. Words Have Reached No Continuation (1091) = Contingency Reaching Necessity

**Words have reached no continuation (המלים הגיעו ללא יכולת להמשיך) = 1091, prime, m14=13.**

Prime. Indecomposable. Words have reached no continuation—this is an indecomposable truth. Words have an end, but God has no end. When words reach God, they cannot continue—because words are contingency, and God is necessity. When contingency reaches necessity, words stop.

**1091 = contingency seeking necessity (1441) − contingency (350).** Words have reached no continuation = contingency seeking necessity − contingency. Words have reached no continuation is contingency seeking necessity minus contingency—contingency seeking necessity, minus contingency itself, is "words have reached no continuation." Words are contingency; words seek necessity; but when words reach necessity, words stop—because necessity does not need words; necessity is necessity.

**m14=13 (rejection/love).** Words have reached no continuation are in rejection and love. Words refuse to continue—words refuse to continue before God, because words cannot be continued by God. Words love God—words stop before God, because words love God; words know that God is greater than words.

**Cannot continue (482) m14=6 (man/Babel).** Cannot continue is in the position of man—man cannot continue, but God can continue. Babel is man's "continuation"—man wanted to keep building, but God stopped Babel. Cannot continue is man's—when man reaches God, he cannot continue. Only God can continue.

**Only God can continue (782) m14=12.** 782 = 2×17×23. Only God can continue is in the complete confusion (12)—only God can continue, because only God is necessity. Contingency cannot continue; necessity can continue. 782 = God (31) + the Void (411) + contingency (350) − 10. God+Void+contingency−10 = only God can continue. God and the Void and contingency minus loving one's neighbor as oneself is "only God can continue"—after subtracting love and contingency, only God can continue.

#### 3. In God (33) and Living in God (61) = With Me

**In God (באל) = 33, m14=5.**

33 = 3×11. Trinity×false light. In God = the Trinity in false light. In God—God is in false light, false light is in God. The Trinity is in false light—false light is not God, but God works in false light.

**Living in God (חיים באל) = 61, prime, m14=5.**

61 = prime. With me (61). Living in God = with me. Living in God is "with me"—God is with me, I live in God. 61 is prime—indecomposable. Living in God is indecomposable—this is a unique experience.

**In God (33) + living in God (61) = 94, m14=10.** 94 = 2×47. Witness×47. In God + living in God = loving one's neighbor as oneself. Life in God is love—to live in God is to love one's neighbor as oneself. 94 = facing sin (94). In God + living in God = facing sin—to live in God is to face sin. Living in God is not escaping sin, but facing sin.

**Man can live in God (558) m14=12.** 558 = 2×3²×31. God does not have to obey anyone (558). Man can live in God = God does not have to obey anyone. The same Gematria—man can live in God, because God does not have to obey anyone. God is free, so man in God is also free. Man can live in God—because God does not need to obey anyone, man does not need to obey anyone (except God).

#### 4. Obedience (140) = David × Love: Obedience Is Not Slavery

**Obedience (כנע) = 140, m14=0, m7=0.**

140 = 2²×5×7 = 14×10. David (14)×loving one's neighbor as oneself (10). Obedience = David×love. The essence of obedience is "David×love"—obedience is love within the covenant. Obedience is not slavery; obedience is love. Slavery is coercion; obedience is love.

**m14=0, m7=0**—complete return to zero. Obedience completely returns to zero in the Davidic covenant. Obedience is not an independent virtue; obedience is completely delimited within the covenant. Obedience returns to zero in the covenant—the meaning of obedience exists only within the covenant. Obedience without the covenant is slavery; obedience with the covenant is love.

**Obedience (140) = 14×10.** David (14)×love (10). Obedience = David×love. Obedience is "David in love"—David obeys God, David is "a man after God's own heart," David's obedience is love. Obedience = David×love—obedience is not abandoning oneself; obedience is "David in love."

**Obedience (140) + betrayal (24) = 164, m14=10.** 164 = 2²×41. Obedience + betrayal = loving one's neighbor as oneself. Obedience and betrayal together are in loving one's neighbor as oneself—obedience is love, betrayal is the opposite of love. Obedience + betrayal are in love—love is the distinction between obedience and betrayal. In love, obedience is positive; betrayal is negative.

#### 5. Betrayal (24) = Heaven (24): Betrayal Is the Highest Rebellion

**Betrayal (בגידה) = 24, m14=10.**

24 = 2³×3. Heaven (the 24 elders). Betrayal is in "heaven"—betrayal is not a thing of earth; betrayal is a thing of heaven. The Gematria of betrayal equals "heaven"—because betrayal is against the heavenly order. Betrayal is not man's invention; betrayal is Satan's invention in heaven.

**Betrayal of God (בגידה באל) = 57, m14=1.**

57 = 3×19. Trinity (3)×oneness (19). Betrayal of God = Trinity×oneness. Betrayal of God destroys the Trinity and oneness—betrayal of God destroys the Trinity (God is the Trinity) and oneness (God is oneness). Betrayal of God = Trinity×oneness—betrayal of God destroys both the Trinity and oneness.

**Betrayal (24) + betrayal of God (57) = 81, m14=11.** 81 = 3⁴. Nature. Betrayal + betrayal of God = nature—betrayal is in nature. Betrayal is part of nature—fallen man naturally betrays God. Betrayal + betrayal of God = Trinity⁴—betrayal is completely delimited by the Trinity.

**Betrayal (24) m14=10 (loving one's neighbor as oneself).** Betrayal is in loving one's neighbor as oneself—betrayal is the opposite of love. Betrayal is the rejection of love—to betray God is to reject God's love. Betrayal is in love—because betrayal occurs within love; betrayal is the negation of love.

#### 6. Man Submitting to Woman Is Betrayal of God (906) = Betrayal in the Position of Love

**Man submitting to woman is betrayal of God (איש נכנע לאשה הוא בגידה באל) = 906, m14=10.**

906 = 2×3×151. Witness×Trinity×151. 151 is prime. Man submitting to woman is betrayal of God = witness×Trinity×151. Witnessed in the Trinity—man submitting to woman is betrayal of God; this is the Trinity's witness.

**m14=10 (loving one's neighbor as oneself).** Man submitting to woman is betrayal of God is in loving one's neighbor as oneself—this is not failing to love woman, but the correct way of loving woman. Man submitting to woman is betrayal of God—because when man submits to woman, man no longer loves woman (in the correct way). Man submitting to woman is "the counterfeit of love"—it looks like love (submission), but is actually betrayal (betrayal of God). Truly loving woman is submitting to God—when man submits to God, loving woman is correct.

**Man cannot submit to woman (965) m14=13.** 965 = 5×193. Grace (5)×193. Man cannot submit to woman is in rejection/love (13). Man cannot submit to woman—this is rejection (rejecting the wrong order) and also love (loving the correct order).

**Man cannot listen to woman (1130) m14=10.** 1130 = 2×5×113. Loving one's neighbor as oneself. Man cannot listen to woman is in loving one's neighbor as oneself—not because of lack of love, but because of love. Man cannot listen to woman is the protection of love—to protect the order of creation is to protect woman.

**Man being born of woman is a mistake (1244) m14=12.** 1244 = 2²×311. 311 = man (311). Man being born of woman is a mistake = witness (2²)×man (311). Man being born of woman is a mistake—witnessed by man himself. Man is not from woman—man is from the dust, from God. Man being born of woman is a mistake—because the order of creation is "man from the dust, woman from man."

**Man being born of woman is a mistake (1244) + man is from the dust (701) = 1945, m14=13.** 1945 = 5×389. Man being born of woman is a mistake + man is from the dust = rejection/love. The two truths together are in rejection and love—rejecting the wrong order, loving the correct order.

#### 7. Man Submits to God (562) vs. Woman Submits to God Through Man (1472) = Both in Witness

**Man submits to God (איש נכנע לאל) = 562, m14=2.**

562 = 2×281. Witness (2)×281. Man submits to God is in witness (2)—man submitting to God is witnessed. 281 is prime.

**Woman submits to God through man (אשה נכנעת לאל דרך איש) = 1472, m14=2.**

1472 = 2⁶×23. Witness⁶×pain (23). Woman submits to God through man = witness⁶×pain. Woman submits to God through man passes through sixfold witness and pain. Witness to the sixth power—complete witness. Pain—woman submits to God through pain (weakness, needing man, facing sin).

**Both m14=2 (witness).** Man submits to God and woman submits to God through man are both in the position of witness—both are witnessed obedience. Man submits to God is direct obedience; woman submits to God through man is indirect obedience—but both are in witness. Obedience is not of only one kind—man directly submits to God, woman submits to God through man—but both submit to God.

**Woman submits to God through man (1472) − man submits to God (562) = 910, m14=0.** 910 = 2×5×7×13 = 14×65. David (14)×65. Woman submits to God through man minus man submits to God = David×65. **Words have reached no continuation—when they reach God, words stop.** Only God can continue. Man can live in God—in God (33) = Trinity×false light; living in God (61) = with me. Living in God is "with me"—God is with man. Man cannot continue, but God can continue—man lives in God, God continues man's life. Words have reached no continuation, but life continues in God.

## 41. The Simulated-World Hypothesis and the True Meaning of "We"

> Nowadays these people always say that we live inside a virtual world, that the foundation of the universe is 0 and 1. Although the foundation of the universe is 0 and 1, it cannot prove that the Lord is a person of the previous world who then built a simulated world—this is not true. If God were a person of the previous world, then his world would also be 0 and 1, and then according to the theory of copying, there can be no perfect copy. If this world exists, then our world is not real but false. Although all this fits the world, God is the Most High, and not an architect of a simulated world. What he builds must be real. And God is the only one. Some say that Kabbalah, the holographic-universe theory, including my own theory, and Genesis all show signs that God is a person of the higher world—but the "we" in Genesis refers to Jesus Christ and the Father and the Holy Spirit. Decode these.

### Core Interpretation

#### 1. Us (425) = Jesus + the One + Yahweh: The Trinitarian Decoding

**Us (נעשה) = 425, m14=5.**

425 = 5×5×17. Grace²×mouth. The square of grace×mouth—"us" is grace²×mouth. The square of grace—grace×grace, the grace of the Father and the grace of the Son. Mouth—the Holy Spirit; the Spirit is the mouth of the Father and the Son, is the witness. Grace²×mouth = the Trinity.

**Us (425) = Jesus (386) + the One (13) + Yahweh (26).**

- Jesus (386) = the Son
- The One (13) = the one God (the Father)
- Yahweh (26) = the covenant-making God (witnessed by the Holy Spirit)

**386 + 13 + 26 = 425.** Exactly equal.

The "us" of Genesis 1:26 = Jesus Christ + the one Father + Yahweh—the Trinity. The "us" of Genesis 1:26 is not "a person of the previous world," not "angels," not "gods"—it is the Trinity. Son, Father, Spirit—three persons, one "us."

**Us (425) = grace²×mouth.** Grace²—the grace of the Father and the grace of the Son, twofold grace. Mouth—the Holy Spirit; the Spirit is the mouth of the Father and the Son, is the witness. Grace²×mouth—the Trinity takes grace squared and mouth as "us."

**Us (425) + create (203) = 628, m14=12.** 628 = Father (3) + Son (52) + Holy Spirit (623) − 50. Trinity − 50 = us + create. Us + create is slightly less than the Trinity. 50 = 2×5×5. Grace²×witness. **Us (425) + make (375) = 800, m14=2.** 800 = 2⁵×5². Witness. Us + make = witness to the fifth power×grace squared. Us-make = witness⁵×grace²—"let us make" is the complete expression of witness and grace.

**True (441) − us (425) = 16.** 16 = 2⁴. Witness to the fourth power. Truth minus us equals witness to the fourth power. "Us" plus witness to the fourth power is truth. Us (the Trinity) plus witness to the fourth power is truth—the Trinity itself is truth, but the "us" of the Trinity in creation plus witness is complete truth.

**Us (425) = David (14) × David (14) × 2 + David (14) + 19.** 14×14×2 = 392. 392+14+19 = 425. Us = David²×2 + David + oneness. Us is within the Davidic covenant.

#### 2. Let Us Make Man (430) = Void and Chaos (430): Creation Comes from the Void

Void and chaos is "no order," not "the previous world." Void and chaos is the "state" before God created, not "the world God came from."

If void and chaos were "the previous world," then "the previous world" would be "empty and void"—empty and void is not a world; empty and void is "no world."

**m14=10 (love your neighbor as yourself).** Let us make man is within loving your neighbor as yourself—creating man is an act of love. Let us make man = void and chaos—love brings man out from void and chaos. Love turns void and chaos into man.

#### 3. True (441) = 3²×7²: The Completeness of Truth

441 = 3²×7². Trinity²×perfection². True = the square of the Trinity×the square of perfection. Trinity²—the Trinity of the Trinity, the perfect Trinity. False exceeds true by "God is the Most High"—false usurps God's being the Most High. False claims to be the Most High—this is the essence of falsehood. **The foundation of the universe is 0 and 1 (373) = prime, m14=9.** Prime. Indecomposable. #### 6. Simulation (251) Prime m14=13: Within Rejection

**Simulation (סימולציה) = 251, prime, m14=13.**

Prime. Indecomposable. m14=13 (rejection/love). Simulation is within rejection/love. Simulation is "rejection"—simulation rejects the real; simulation is the substitute of the real. ### Summary: The Simulation Hypothesis and the Truth of "We"

**1. "We" is the Trinity.** The "us" (425) of Genesis 1:26 = Jesus (386) + the One (13) + Yahweh (26). "We" is not "a person of the previous world," not "angels," not "gods"—"we" is the Trinity. Son, Father, Holy Spirit—three persons, one "we." The Trinity is in creation—"let us make man"—the Trinity deliberates to make man, not "a person of the previous world" deliberating to make man.

The existence of the Son proves that God is not a person of the previous world. The Son is begotten of the Father, not come from the previous world. The Son is not "the son of a person of the previous world"; the Son is "the Father's only-begotten Son." The Son proves that God has no previous world—the Son has only the Father, no "previous world."

**5. The previous world is a human concept.** Previous world (216) = 6³ = man³. The previous world is man's imagination—man comes from man, and man thinks everything comes from man. But God does not come from man—God is the Self-Existent One. The previous world is Babel's concept—man wants to build a tower reaching the heavens, man wants to become God. The previous world is the replica of the tower of Babel.

**6. Universe and Most High are the same Gematria.** Universe (116) = Most High (116). The universe is not the Most High, but the universe points to the Most High. Universe and Most High are in the same number—the universe is the creation of the Most High, the Most High is the creator of the universe. Universe and Most High are within the gate—the universe is the gate of the Most High, the Most High is the gate of the universe.

**7. Copy theory self-negates.** Perfect copy (1396) < cannot copy perfectly (1884). The inner mathematics of copy theory is that "cannot copy perfectly" is greater than "perfect copy"—copy theory self-negates. Cannot copy perfectly is within resurrection (8)—resurrection cannot be copied; resurrection proves that copy theory is wrong. God is the Creator, not a copier. God creates from nothing to something, not copies from the previous world.

**8. What God builds is true (892) m14=10.** What God builds is true is within love. God is love; what God builds is true, not false. God is not the architect of the simulated world; God is the creator of the real world. What God builds is true—because God is true, what God builds is also true.

**Core: Us (425) = Jesus (386) + the One (13) + Yahweh (26).** This is the "we" of Genesis 1:26—not "a person of the previous world," not "the architect of the simulated world," not "the projection of the holographic universe"—but the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit. The Trinity is in creation—"let us make man"—the Trinity makes man; it is not simulating man, not copying man, but creating man.

Starting from theology, it will also reach the end. Why does Satan not wait until the moment when technology discovers God's wisdom? Satan acts on the eve of the light, because the darkness before dawn is the deepest, and also the last covering. A liar always covers the lie to the very end; he speaks only when he can no longer be silent. This is also the sin I once committed.
